BUILDIT | COMPLETE PROJECT TEXT

This file is a shareable text export of the Buildit carbon aware city planner. It contains a plain language guide to the app, its visible screens and controls, data model, optimization behavior, project structure, and the full text of the project documentation, source, data, and configuration files. Third party dependencies in node_modules, generated production files in dist, and binary assets are not included.

============================================================
1. PROJECT OVERVIEW
============================================================

Buildit is an interactive 3D city planning app. People place buildings and infrastructure on a 40 by 40 grid, inspect modeled carbon and cost estimates, and ask the optimizer to propose a lower carbon city. The app uses 3D low poly models and presents the original and optimized layouts side by side.

The project is built with React 19, TypeScript, Vite 8, Tailwind CSS 4, Three.js, React Three Fiber, Drei, Recharts, and Zustand. The app uses procedural 3D geometry for city assets. The city grid, asset values, calculations, user interface, and optimizer are implemented in the included source files.

============================================================
2. WHAT THE USER SEES
============================================================

HEADER
• Product label: CITY CARBON PLANNER
• Product name: Buildit
• Reset city: clears the city.
• Load trial city: loads a busy sample city for trying the optimizer.
• Sources: opens source values and assumptions.
• Compare: switches to the side by side comparison view.
• A score badge shows a carbon grade when the city has residential assets.

BUILD PALETTE
The left panel says Build palette and Choose a city asset. It shows the number of available assets and a button for each asset type:
1. Residential
2. Office
3. Road
4. Metro
5. Park
6. Solar farm
7. Industry
8. Water body
9. School
10. Hospital
11. Wastewater plant
12. Data center
13. Hotel
14. Shopping mall
15. Landfill
16. Urban forest
17. Wind turbine
18. Diesel bus depot

Each asset tile shows its cost in crore rupees, embodied carbon or Embodied n/a when not modeled, and its grid footprint. Selecting an asset makes it the active placement tool and highlights matching placed assets in the city scene. The highlight uses a bright footprint outline and a subtle color wash. The eraser removes a whole asset, including all tiles of a larger asset. The palette lists shortcuts for the first nine assets. Press E to select the eraser.

CITY CANVAS
The central panel is an interactive 3D view of the city grid. The scene legend lists all asset types. The active tool appears at the top. Paint drag can be turned on to keep dragging across empty tiles to place multiple assets. The tile inspector shows the row, column, and asset under the pointer. The canvas help says either Hold and drag to place, Scroll to zoom, Right click to erase, or Drag to orbit, Scroll to zoom, Right click to erase. The user can orbit and zoom the camera. Assets appear at their grid position and use their listed footprint, including 1 by 1, 2 by 2, and 3 by 3 tiles.

LIVE IMPACT PANEL
The right panel is titled Live impact model and shows a city outlook over the model horizon. It displays carbon intensity per capita and a meter from High impact to Net zero. Metric cards show Total carbon, Embodied, Operations + transit, Offset, Capital cost, and Net energy. Values are estimates. A carbon trajectory chart separates embodied emissions, operating emissions, and offsets across time.

BOTTOM CONTROLS
• A play or pause button runs the carbon timeline.
• A slider selects the projection year from year 0 to the model horizon.
• Budget accepts a spending limit in crore rupees. Optimize runs the budget based optimizer.
• Target reduction accepts a percentage. Optimize target tries to reach that reduction or reports the closest result it finds.
• Find minimum searches for the lowest carbon result allowed by the current model, asset limits, and available grid space.

COMPARISON VIEW
View 1 follows city edits and saves the original layout. View 2 shows the optimized layout after an optimization. Each side includes a 3D preview, total carbon over the model horizon, embodied emissions, operations and transport, offset, and capital cost. A summary reports the carbon difference, how many existing assets moved, whether a reduction target was reached, and the model horizon. Existing buildings are retained. They may be repositioned.

SOURCES PANEL
The Sources button opens a searchable table titled Model sources and calculated values. It shows each input name, value, unit, source status, basis, and links where available. Statuses distinguish cited values, candidate values, assumptions, and placeholders. The panel also lists calculated per asset values and assumptions for footprints and optimizer limits. Search accepts asset names, sources, and assumptions.

OPTIMIZATION NOTICES
If a requested reduction cannot be reached, the app reports the requested and best achieved percentages, the remaining gap, and that some emissions cannot be changed by the current asset mix. It lists assets added and existing assets repositioned. It suggests modeling efficiency or direct emissions upgrades, expanding clean energy options or limits, or revising assumptions. The current model allows at most one new wind turbine per run.

If Find minimum completes without reaching zero emissions, the notice reports the lowest result found with the current model and explains that “Minimum” means the lowest result found within the current grid, asset limits, and carbon assumptions.

============================================================
3. CITY GRID AND ASSETS
============================================================

The city has 40 rows and 40 columns. Grid placement validates boundaries, asset footprints, collisions, and complete asset deletion. Multi tile assets are tracked by their origin so adjacent identical assets remain distinct. Asset models are scaled to their footprints. The metro icon uses a train track symbol.

The asset catalog contains 18 asset types: Residential, Office, Road, Metro, Park, Solar farm, Industry, Water body, School, Hospital, Wastewater plant, Data center, Hotel, Shopping mall, Landfill, Urban forest, Wind turbine, and Diesel bus depot. Their footprints range from 1 by 1 to 3 by 3. Exact costs, embodied carbon, annual energy, direct emissions, carbon uptake, capacities, footprints, assumptions, and sources are defined in src/data/items.ts and src/data/carbon-inputs.json and reproduced in full in the source appendix below.

The trial city is randomized when loaded. It starts with 410 occupied tiles and uses a dense mixture of city assets to give the optimizer a meaningful scenario. Roads provide fixed infrastructure in optimizer runs.

============================================================
4. CARBON ESTIMATES
============================================================

The model horizon is 30 years. Carbon values are estimates based on cited research inputs, candidate values, and explicit assumptions. The model calculates embodied carbon, electricity emissions, direct emissions, transport emissions, carbon sinks, generated electricity, net energy, and capital cost.

Where possible, embodied carbon is calculated from floor area multiplied by embodied carbon intensity. Annual energy is calculated from area and energy intensity. Consumed grid energy is multiplied by the grid emissions factor. Direct pond or wastewater emissions are added where modeled. Solar and wind generation, park sinks, and urban forest uptake reduce the modeled total. Metro and parks can change modeled emissions for nearby homes and energy users according to the supplied multipliers. Costs are summed per asset, not per occupied grid cell.

The Sources table documents assumptions and uncertainty. Some assets have no modeled construction carbon; this is marked as unmodeled and does not mean construction is carbon free. Water body emissions use an urban pond proxy and depend on climate and water quality. Other facility values scale research benchmarks to assumed tile areas or facility designs. Results are not measured site inventories.

============================================================
5. OPTIMIZATION
============================================================

The optimizer scores candidate placements using the modeled 30 year carbon benefit and asset cost, checks footprint placement and available space, and favors useful locations. Solar is favored near large electricity users. Parks and forests are favored near homes and offices. Metro is favored near residential assets. It may reposition eligible existing assets when the modeled result improves. Roads are not added or moved, and existing buildings are not deleted.

Budget optimization treats the entered amount as the maximum additional capital spending. Budget mode and target or minimum mode now use the same maximum additions per run: 75 solar farms, 1 wind turbine, 40 urban forests, 40 parks, and 8 metro stops. Budget mode can stop sooner when the budget, free space, footprint rules, or modeled benefit prevents further additions. The optimization runs in a Web Worker when supported so the interface remains responsive.

Target optimization adds useful assets until it reaches the requested percentage or cannot find a valid additional placement that improves the result. Find minimum uses the same placement strategy and limits but searches for the lowest result it can find. All optimization results are saved to comparison View 2, with the starting city in View 1.

============================================================
6. PROJECT FILES
============================================================

src/App.tsx mounts the main layout.
src/components/ contains the header, asset palette, 3D canvas and models, statistics, carbon chart, source table, comparison view, and timeline controls.
src/data/items.ts defines the 18 assets and derives their model values.
src/data/carbon-inputs.json stores numeric inputs, units, references, URLs, years, and source statuses.
src/lib/grid.ts handles grid creation, asset placement, footprints, deletion, and asset listing.
src/lib/carbon.ts calculates modeled carbon and related metrics.
src/lib/optimizer.ts contains placement scoring, asset additions, and repositioning logic.
src/lib/optimizer.worker.ts runs optimization in a background worker.
src/store/useCityStore.ts keeps city state, edits, saved layouts, and optimization state.
src/index.css contains global styles and the color theme.
index.html is the HTML shell. package.json and pnpm-lock.yaml define the JavaScript project and dependency versions. vite.config.ts configures the development and production build.

Useful project commands:
pnpm dev starts the development server.
pnpm test runs the grid and optimizer test suite.
pnpm build creates the production build.

============================================================
7. FULL PROJECT TEXT
============================================================
The following sections contain each first party documentation, source, data, and configuration file as plain text. node_modules, dist, Git metadata, and binary files are intentionally omitted.

# figma-make-app

React + Vite + Tailwind CSS project running inside Figma Make.

## Development Server

A Vite development server is **already running** on `$PORT` (default 8443). You don't need to start it manually.

- Preview URL: The user can access the running app through the preview panel
- Hot reload: Changes to source files are reflected immediately

## Project Structure

This is the canonical project structure. Start with task-relevant files below. Only follow imports or inspect other files when required, when a documented path is missing, or when the repository contradicts this guide.

- `src/main.tsx` - React entrypoint; imports `src/index.css` and mounts `src/App.tsx` into the `#root` element
- `src/App.tsx` - Primary application component and the usual starting point for UI work
- `src/index.css` - Global CSS entrypoint and Tailwind CSS v4 import
- `index.html` - Vite HTML shell containing the `#root` element and loading `src/main.tsx`
- `package.json` - Project dependencies and the Vite build, development, preview, and formatting scripts
- `vite.config.ts` - Vite configuration with React, Tailwind CSS v4, and Figma Make plugins plus the `@` alias for `src`
- `.mise.toml` - Toolchain versions for Node.js and pnpm

## Dependencies

- Runtime: React 19 and React DOM 19
- Styling: Tailwind CSS v4 with the `@tailwindcss/vite` plugin
- Build tooling: Vite 8, TypeScript 5.7, and `@vitejs/plugin-react`
- Formatting: oxfmt

## Styling

This project uses **Tailwind CSS v4** through the `@tailwindcss/vite` plugin configured in `vite.config.ts`. `src/index.css` imports Tailwind with `@import 'tailwindcss';`. Use Tailwind utility classes directly in JSX and put global CSS or Tailwind v4 theme customization in `src/index.css`. This scaffold does not need a Tailwind config file or PostCSS config.

`src/main.tsx` imports `src/index.css`, so global font wiring belongs in `src/index.css`. Keep CSS `@import` statements first, then add any `@font-face` rules and font-family defaults there.

## Code quality

- Use double quotes for strings containing apostrophes (`"We're here to help"`), or escape them in single-quoted strings. An unescaped apostrophe in a single-quoted string breaks the build.
- Ensure JSX tags are closed and braces are balanced.
- Export components as default exports.

================================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================================

# Carbon-Aware 3D City Builder

An interactive 40 × 40 low-poly city planner with an eighteen-asset palette, carbon and cost estimates, comparison layouts, a projection timeline, and budget- or target-based carbon optimization.

## Run and validate

The Figma Make development server is already running. Project scripts:

```bash
pnpm dev
pnpm test
pnpm build
```

## City model

`src/store/useCityStore.ts` owns the grid and user actions. Single-cell assets are stored as their item ID. Multi-tile assets use an origin-tagged value in each occupied cell, which keeps adjacent identical assets distinct. `src/lib/grid.ts` validates footprints, places and erases whole assets, and lists each asset once for rendering and calculations.

The 3D canvas lives in `src/components/CityScene.tsx`. It reads the shared store, maps grid rows to world-space depth and columns to world-space width, renders each asset at its footprint center, and sends ground clicks back through the store. `src/components/CanvasPlaceholder.tsx` retains the palette-independent scene overlays and inspector.

## Carbon model and sources

`src/data/carbon-inputs.json` preserves the carbon input table shared by the user. `src/data/items.ts` derives per-asset embodied carbon and energy from area and intensity where the table provides those values. `src/lib/carbon.ts` applies the grid factor, transport emissions, metro and park adjacency assumptions, and the shared projection horizon. Every asset contributes embodied carbon, energy, and cost once, regardless of its occupied cell count.

The searchable Sources panel shows raw inputs, calculated values, source links, and candidate/assumption status. Factory embodied carbon and building energy use published Indian factory and railway-workshop benchmarks. Water-body direct emissions use a measured urban-pond flux proxy; its area is assumed, construction carbon is excluded, and climate/water-quality differences make the result uncertain. School, hospital, hotel, and mall energy use published Indian benchmarks. Wastewater energy and fugitive emissions use surveyed Indian treatment-plant values scaled to an assumed 10 MLD plant; data-center electricity is calculated from India's published load/utilization/PUE method with an illustrative 1 MW IT load. New hotel, mall, landfill, forest, wind, and diesel-depot values use published rates scaled to the documented design assumptions. Construction carbon for these assets is unmodeled and is labeled that way. Budget optimization retains its budget limit; target optimization greedily adds low-carbon assets until the requested 30-year reduction is reached or the model cannot find another helpful legal placement. Neither optimizer removes existing assets, and both save the original city in compare View 1 and the result in View 2.

## Main files

- `src/components/` — 3D scene, palette, metrics, charts, comparison, and sources UI
- `src/data/carbon-inputs.json` — supplied source values, units, references, and statuses
- `src/data/items.ts` — asset catalog and calculated model values
- `src/lib/grid.ts` — placement, footprint, origin, and erase logic
- `src/lib/carbon.ts` — carbon, energy, cost, population, and horizon calculations
- `src/lib/optimizer.ts` — footprint-aware budget optimizer
- `src/store/useCityStore.ts` — city and scenario state
- `src/lib/grid.test.ts` — grid, accounting, and optimizer tests

import { defineConfig, type HtmlTagDescriptor, type Plugin } from 'vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'
import path from 'node:path'

import siteConfiguration from './.figma/make/site.json'


// Vite config — https://vitejs.dev/config/
export default defineConfig(({ mode }) => {
  // .figma/make/deploy-preview passes `--mode development` for cached-preview builds.
  const emitSourcemaps = mode === 'development'

  return {
    base: process.env.FIGMA_PUBLIC_URL ? `${process.env.FIGMA_PUBLIC_URL}/` : '/',
    build: {
      sourcemap: emitSourcemaps ? 'inline' : false,
      minify: !emitSourcemaps,
      rolldownOptions: {
        output: {
          codeSplitting: {
            groups: [{
              name: 'vendor',
              test: /node_modules/,
              minSize: 50000,
              maxSize: 350000,
              priority: 10,
            }],
          },
        },
      },
    },
    plugins: [
react(),
      tailwindcss(),
      figmaSiteConfiguration(siteConfiguration),
      figmaErrorOverlayReplay(),
      figmaReactRefreshBoundaryFallback(),
      figmaMakeKitPlugin({ storiesGlob: '/src/**/*.stories.{ts,tsx,js,jsx}' }),
    ],
    resolve: {
      alias: {
        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, './src'),
      },
    },
    server: {
      host: process.env.FIGMA_DEV_SERVER_HOST || '0.0.0.0',
      port: parseInt(process.env.PORT || '8443'),
      strictPort: true,
      watch: {
        ignored: [
          '**/.figma/**',
],
      },
    },
    preview: {
      host: process.env.FIGMA_DEV_SERVER_HOST || '0.0.0.0',
      port: parseInt(process.env.PORT || '8443'),
    },
  }
})

type FigmaSiteConfiguration = {
  title?: string
  description?: string
  language?: string
  robots?: {
    index?: boolean
  }
  icons?: {
    icon?: string
  }
  openGraph?: {
    image?: string
  }
  analytics?: {
    googleAnalyticsId?: string
  }
  customScripts?: {
    headStart?: string
    headEnd?: string
    bodyStart?: string
    bodyEnd?: string
  }
  accessibility?: {
    addBypassLinks?: boolean
  }
}

/** Applies /.figma/make/site.json to the generated document shell. */
function figmaSiteConfiguration(config: FigmaSiteConfiguration): Plugin {
  function sanitizeHtmlValue(value: string | undefined): string {
    return value?.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '') || ''
  }
  function escapeHtmlText(value: string): string {
    return value.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
  }
  function replaceHtmlCommentSlot(html: string, slotName: string, content: string): string {
    return html.replace(`<!-- ${slotName} -->`, content)
  }

  const title = config.title ?? "Buildit"
  const description = config.description ?? ''
  const favicon = config.icons?.icon ?? ''
  const socialImage = config.openGraph?.image ?? ''
  const language = sanitizeHtmlValue(config.language) || 'en'
  const googleAnalyticsId = sanitizeHtmlValue(config.analytics?.googleAnalyticsId)
  const headStart = config.customScripts?.headStart ?? ''
  const headEnd = config.customScripts?.headEnd ?? ''
  const bodyStart = config.customScripts?.bodyStart ?? ''
  const bodyEnd = config.customScripts?.bodyEnd ?? ''
  const robotsTxt = config.robots?.index === false ? 'User-agent: *\nDisallow: /\n' : ''

  return {
    name: 'figma-site-configuration',
    configureServer(server) {
      server.middlewares.use((req, res, next) => {
        if (!robotsTxt || req.url?.split('?')[0] !== '/robots.txt') return next()

        res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/plain; charset=utf-8')
        res.end(robotsTxt)
      })
    },
    generateBundle() {
      if (!robotsTxt) return

      this.emitFile({
        type: 'asset',
        fileName: 'robots.txt',
        source: robotsTxt,
      })
    },
    transformIndexHtml: {
      order: 'pre',
      handler(html) {
        let result = html
        result = replaceHtmlCommentSlot(result, 'figma:lang', language)
        result = replaceHtmlCommentSlot(result, 'figma:title', escapeHtmlText(title))
        result = replaceHtmlCommentSlot(result, 'figma:head-start', headStart)
        result = replaceHtmlCommentSlot(result, 'figma:head-end', headEnd)
        result = replaceHtmlCommentSlot(result, 'figma:body-start', bodyStart)
        result = replaceHtmlCommentSlot(result, 'figma:body-end', bodyEnd)

        const tags: HtmlTagDescriptor[] = []
        if (description) {
          tags.push({ tag: 'meta', attrs: { name: 'description', content: description }, injectTo: 'head' })
        }
        if (config.robots?.index === false) {
          tags.push({ tag: 'meta', attrs: { name: 'robots', content: 'noindex, nofollow' }, injectTo: 'head' })
        }
        if (favicon) {
          tags.push({ tag: 'link', attrs: { rel: 'icon', href: favicon }, injectTo: 'head' })
        }
        if (title) {
          tags.push({ tag: 'meta', attrs: { property: 'og:title', content: title }, injectTo: 'head' })
        }
        if (description) {
          tags.push({ tag: 'meta', attrs: { property: 'og:description', content: description }, injectTo: 'head' })
        }
        if (socialImage) {
          tags.push(
            { tag: 'meta', attrs: { property: 'og:image', content: socialImage }, injectTo: 'head' },
            { tag: 'meta', attrs: { name: 'twitter:card', content: 'summary_large_image' }, injectTo: 'head' },
            { tag: 'meta', attrs: { name: 'twitter:image', content: socialImage }, injectTo: 'head' },
          )
        }

        if (googleAnalyticsId) {
          tags.push(
            {
              tag: 'script',
              attrs: {
                async: true,
                src: `https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtag/js?id=${googleAnalyticsId}`,
              },
              injectTo: 'head',
            },
            {
              tag: 'script',
              children: `
  window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
  function gtag(){dataLayer.push(arguments);}
  gtag('js', new Date());
  gtag('config', ${JSON.stringify(googleAnalyticsId)});
`,
              injectTo: 'head',
            },
          )
        }

        if (config.accessibility?.addBypassLinks) {
          tags.push(
            {
              tag: 'style',
              children: `
  .figma-bypass-link {
    position: fixed;
    top: 8px;
    left: 8px;
    z-index: 2147483647;
    transform: translateY(-150%);
    border-radius: 6px;
    background: #111827;
    color: #fff;
    padding: 8px 12px;
    font: 600 14px/1.2 system-ui, sans-serif;
    text-decoration: none;
  }
  .figma-bypass-link:focus {
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
`,
              injectTo: 'head',
            },
            {
              tag: 'a',
              attrs: { class: 'figma-bypass-link', href: '#root' },
              children: 'Skip to content',
              injectTo: 'body-prepend',
            },
          )
        }

        return {
          html: result,
          tags,
        }
      },
    },
  }
}

/**
 * Replay the most recent build error to clients that connect after
 * it was first broadcast. Vite buffers an error payload only while
 * no clients are connected and clears the buffer on the first
 * reconnect (see `bufferedMessage` in `createWebSocketServer`), so
 * if the preview iframe reloads after Vite already delivered an
 * error to a live socket, the new socket misses the payload and
 * the overlay stays hidden even though the build is still broken.
 * We intercept `ws.send` to remember the latest error and replay
 * it on every new connection; the cache clears on a successful
 * `update` or `full-reload` so a stale overlay can't survive a
 * fixed build.
 */
function figmaErrorOverlayReplay(): Plugin {
  return {
    name: 'figma-error-overlay-replay',
    apply: 'serve',
    configureServer(server) {
      let lastError: object | null = null

      const origSend = server.ws.send.bind(server.ws) as (...args: any[]) => void
      server.ws.send = ((...args: any[]) => {
        const payload = args[0]
        if (payload && typeof payload === 'object' && !Array.isArray(payload)) {
          const type = (payload as { type?: string }).type
          if (type === 'error') {
            lastError = payload as object
          } else if (type === 'update' || type === 'full-reload') {
            lastError = null
          }
        }
        return origSend(...args)
      }) as typeof server.ws.send

      server.ws.on('connection', (socket) => {
        if (lastError !== null) {
          socket.send(JSON.stringify(lastError))
        }
      })
    },
  }
}

/**
 * Reload when a module that previously defined a React Refresh boundary stops
 * defining one. This happens when an agent moves a component into a new file
 * and replaces the old module with a re-export:
 *
 *   export { default } from './app/App'
 *
 * Vite otherwise accepts the update using the previous module's HMR boundary,
 * but the re-export-only transform no longer registers a replacement for the
 * mounted component family. React reports a successful refresh while leaving
 * the old tree mounted until the page is reloaded.
 */
function figmaReactRefreshBoundaryFallback(): Plugin {
  type ModuleNode = import('vite').ModuleNode
  const hadRefreshBoundary = new Map<string, boolean>()
  const lostRefreshBoundaries = new Set<string>()
  let sendFullReload: (() => void) | null = null

  return {
    name: 'figma-react-refresh-boundary-fallback',
    apply: 'serve',
    enforce: 'post',
    configureServer(server) {
      sendFullReload = () => server.ws.send({ type: 'full-reload', path: '*' })
    },
    handleHotUpdate({ modules, server, timestamp }) {
      if (lostRefreshBoundaries.size === 0) return

      const visited = new Set<ModuleNode>()
      const pending = [...modules]
      while (pending.length > 0) {
        const current = pending.pop()
        if (!current || visited.has(current)) continue
        visited.add(current)

        const moduleId = current.id?.split('?')[0]
        if (moduleId && lostRefreshBoundaries.has(moduleId)) {
          const invalidated = new Set<ModuleNode>()
          for (const updatedModule of modules) {
            server.moduleGraph.invalidateModule(updatedModule, invalidated, timestamp, true)
          }
          sendFullReload?.()
          return []
        }

        pending.push(...current.importers)
      }
    },
    transform(code, id) {
      if (!/\.[jt]sx?(?:\?|$)/.test(id) || id.includes('/node_modules/')) return null

      const moduleId = id.split('?')[0] ?? id
      const hasRefreshBoundary = code.includes('registerExportsForReactRefresh')
      const previousHadRefreshBoundary = hadRefreshBoundary.get(moduleId)
      hadRefreshBoundary.set(moduleId, hasRefreshBoundary)

      if (hasRefreshBoundary) lostRefreshBoundaries.delete(moduleId)
      if (previousHadRefreshBoundary && !hasRefreshBoundary) {
        lostRefreshBoundaries.add(moduleId)
        queueMicrotask(() => sendFullReload?.())
      }

      return null
    },
  }
}

/**
 * Serves a blank render-target page at /.figma/make/kit.html that
 * the Figma preview script drives directly. The page exposes a
 * registry of every file matching `storiesGlob` on
 * window.__FIGMA__.stories so the design surface can dynamically
 * import + mount each entry into its own grid view.
 *
 * Dev-only: `apply: 'serve'` gates the plugin to `vite dev`. Prod
 * builds (`vite build`) skip it entirely so the route doesn't leak
 * into shipped bundles.
 */
function figmaMakeKitPlugin(options: { storiesGlob: string | string[] }): Plugin {
  const storiesGlob = Array.isArray(options.storiesGlob) ? options.storiesGlob : [options.storiesGlob]
  const ROUTE = '/.figma/make/kit.html'
  const VIRTUAL_ID = 'virtual:figma-stories'
  const RESOLVED_ID = '\0' + VIRTUAL_ID
  const STORIES_MODULE = `export const stories = import.meta.glob(${JSON.stringify(storiesGlob)})`
  const HTML_BOOTSTRAP = `<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8" />
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0" />
</head>
<body>
<div id="figma-make-kit-root"></div>
<script type="module">
  import { stories } from 'virtual:figma-stories'
  window.__FIGMA__ = Object.assign(window.__FIGMA__ ?? {}, { stories })
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('figma.ready'))
</script>
</body>
</html>`

  return {
    name: 'figma-make-kit',
    apply: 'serve',
    resolveId(id) {
      if (id === VIRTUAL_ID) return RESOLVED_ID
      return null
    },
    load(id) {
      if (id !== RESOLVED_ID) return null
      return STORIES_MODULE
    },
    configureServer(server) {
      server.middlewares.use(async (req, res, next) => {
        const url = req.url || ''
        if (url.split('?')[0] !== ROUTE) return next()

        try {
          res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/html')
          res.end(await server.transformIndexHtml(url, HTML_BOOTSTRAP))
        } catch (err) {
          next(err as Error)
        }
      })
    },
  }
}

FILE: src\components\AnimatedNumber.tsx
================================================================

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react"

type Props = {
  value: number
  decimals?: number
  prefix?: string
}

export default function AnimatedNumber({
  value,
  decimals = 0,
  prefix = "",
}: Props) {
  const [display, setDisplay] = useState(value)
  const previousTarget = useRef(value)

  useEffect(() => {
    const from = previousTarget.current
    const difference = value - from
    const started = performance.now()
    let frame = 0
    const animate = (time: number) => {
      const progress = Math.min((time - started) / 350, 1)
      const eased = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3)
      setDisplay(from + difference * eased)
      if (progress < 1) frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate)
    }
    frame = requestAnimationFrame(animate)
    previousTarget.current = value
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame)
  }, [value])

  return (
    <>
      {prefix}
      {display.toLocaleString("en-IN", {
        maximumFractionDigits: decimals,
        minimumFractionDigits: decimals,
      })}
    </>
  )
}

import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { Crosshair, Layers3 } from "lucide-react"
import { ITEM_IDS, ITEMS, ITEM_SHORTCUTS } from "../data/items"
import { useCityStore } from "../store/useCityStore"
const CityScene = lazy(() => import("./CityScene"))

type HoveredCell = { row: number; col: number; item: string | null } | null

type Props = {
  hoveredCell: HoveredCell
  onHoverCell: (cell: HoveredCell) => void
}

export default function CanvasPlaceholder({ hoveredCell, onHoverCell }: Props) {
  const [paintDrag, setPaintDrag] = useState(false)
  const selectedItem = useCityStore((state) => state.selectedItem)
  const tool = useCityStore((state) => state.tool)
  const setSelectedItem = useCityStore((state) => state.setSelectedItem)
  const setTool = useCityStore((state) => state.setTool)

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.target instanceof HTMLInputElement) return
      if (event.key.toLowerCase() === "e") setTool("erase")
      const itemId = ITEM_IDS.find((id) => ITEM_SHORTCUTS[id] === event.key)
      if (itemId) setSelectedItem(itemId)
    }
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown)
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown)
  }, [setSelectedItem, setTool])

  return (
    <section className="panel relative min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden" aria-label="Interactive 3D city scene">
      <Suspense fallback={<div aria-live="polite" className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center text-sm text-muted">Loading city scene…</div>}>
        <CityScene
          hover={hoveredCell ? { row: hoveredCell.row, col: hoveredCell.col } : null}
          onHover={onHoverCell}
          paintDrag={paintDrag}
        />
      </Suspense>

      <div className="absolute left-4 top-4 z-10 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-line bg-panel/90 px-3 py-2 text-xs shadow-xl backdrop-blur">
        <Crosshair size={14} className="text-accent" aria-hidden="true" />
        <span className="text-muted">Active tool</span>
        <strong className="text-white">{tool === "erase" ? "Eraser" : ITEMS[selectedItem].label}</strong>
        <button
          aria-pressed={paintDrag}
          className={`ml-1 rounded-md border px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold transition ${paintDrag ? "border-accent bg-accent/15 text-accent" : "border-line text-muted hover:border-line-strong hover:text-primary"}`}
          onClick={() => setPaintDrag((enabled) => !enabled)}
          title={paintDrag ? "Turn off drag placement to orbit the camera" : "Hold and drag across empty tiles to place multiple assets"}
          type="button"
        >
          Paint drag {paintDrag ? "On" : "Off"}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute right-4 top-4 z-10 rounded-xl border border-line bg-panel/90 p-3 shadow-xl backdrop-blur">
        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          <Layers3 size={13} aria-hidden="true" /> Scene legend
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-3 gap-y-1.5">
          {ITEM_IDS.map((id) => (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] text-secondary" key={id}>
              <span className="size-2 rounded-sm" style={{ backgroundColor: ITEMS[id].color }} />
              {ITEMS[id].label}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-4 left-4 z-10 min-w-52 rounded-xl border border-line bg-panel/90 p-3 shadow-xl backdrop-blur">
        <div className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">Tile inspector</div>
        <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-white">
          {hoveredCell ? `Row ${hoveredCell.row + 1}, column ${hoveredCell.col + 1}` : "Hover over a tile"}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-dim">
          {hoveredCell?.item ? ITEMS[hoveredCell.item as keyof typeof ITEMS].label : "No asset placed"}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-4 right-4 z-10 rounded-lg border border-line bg-panel/85 px-3 py-2 text-[10px] text-muted">
      {paintDrag ? "Hold and drag to place · Scroll to zoom · Right click to erase" : "Drag to orbit · Scroll to zoom · Right click to erase"}
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}


================================================================
FILE: src\components\CarbonChart.tsx
================================================================

import {
  Area,
  AreaChart,
  CartesianGrid,
  ReferenceLine,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Tooltip,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
} from "recharts"
import { computeCarbon, MODEL_HORIZON, type City } from "../lib/carbon"

export default function CarbonChart({ city }: { city: City }) {
  const data = Array.from({ length: MODEL_HORIZON + 1 }, (_, year) => {
    const result = computeCarbon(city, year)
    return {
      year,
      embodied: Math.round(result.embodied),
      operational: Math.round(result.operational + result.transport),
      offset: -Math.round(result.offset),
    }
  })
  const yearOne = computeCarbon(city, 1)
  const paybackYear =
    yearOne.offset > 0
      ? Math.min(MODEL_HORIZON, Math.ceil(yearOne.embodied / yearOne.offset))
      : null

  return (
    <div className="h-44 min-h-44 min-w-0 w-full">
      <ResponsiveContainer
        height="100%"
        initialDimension={{ height: 176, width: 320 }}
        minHeight={176}
        minWidth={1}
        width="100%"
      >
        <AreaChart
          data={data}
          margin={{ bottom: 0, left: -22, right: 5, top: 8 }}
        >
          <defs>
            <linearGradient id="embodiedFill" x1="0" x2="0" y1="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="5%" stopColor="var(--amber)" stopOpacity={0.35} />
              <stop offset="95%" stopColor="var(--amber)" stopOpacity={0.02} />
            </linearGradient>
            <linearGradient id="operationalFill" x1="0" x2="0" y1="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="5%" stopColor="var(--danger)" stopOpacity={0.32} />
              <stop offset="95%" stopColor="var(--danger)" stopOpacity={0.02} />
            </linearGradient>
            <linearGradient id="offsetFill" x1="0" x2="0" y1="0" y2="1">
              <stop offset="5%" stopColor="var(--emerald)" stopOpacity={0.02} />
              <stop
                offset="95%"
                stopColor="var(--emerald)"
                stopOpacity={0.35}
              />
            </linearGradient>
          </defs>
          <CartesianGrid
            stroke="var(--line)"
            strokeDasharray="3 5"
            vertical={false}
          />
          <XAxis
            axisLine={false}
            dataKey="year"
            fontSize={10}
            stroke="var(--dim)"
            tickLine={false}
            ticks={[0, 10, 20, MODEL_HORIZON]}
          />
          <YAxis
            axisLine={false}
            fontSize={9}
            stroke="var(--dim)"
            tickFormatter={(value) => `${Math.round(value / 1000)}k`}
            tickLine={false}
          />
          <Tooltip
            contentStyle={{
              background: "var(--panel-solid)",
              border: "1px solid var(--line-strong)",
              borderRadius: "10px",
              color: "var(--primary)",
              fontSize: "11px",
            }}
            formatter={(value) => [
              `${Number(value).toLocaleString()} tCO₂`,
              "",
            ]}
            labelFormatter={(label) => `Year ${label}`}
          />
          <Area
            dataKey="embodied"
            fill="url(#embodiedFill)"
            name="Embodied"
            stackId="positive"
            stroke="var(--amber)"
            strokeWidth={2}
            type="monotone"
          />
          <Area
            dataKey="operational"
            fill="url(#operationalFill)"
            name="Operational"
            stackId="positive"
            stroke="var(--danger)"
            strokeWidth={2}
            type="monotone"
          />
          <Area
            dataKey="offset"
            fill="url(#offsetFill)"
            name="Offset"
            stroke="var(--emerald)"
            strokeWidth={2}
            type="monotone"
          />
          {paybackYear ? (
            <ReferenceLine
              label={{
                fill: "var(--emerald)",
                fontSize: 9,
                position: "insideTopRight",
                value: `Payback Y${paybackYear}`,
              }}
              stroke="var(--emerald)"
              strokeDasharray="4 4"
              x={paybackYear}
            />
          ) : null}
        </AreaChart>
      </ResponsiveContainer>
    </div>
  )
}

function WindModel() {
  return <group>
    <mesh position-y={0.06} receiveShadow><boxGeometry args={[1.94, 0.12, 1.94]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#77776e" /></mesh>

      <mesh castShadow><sphereGeometry args={[0.13, 10, 8]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#c6c3b7" metalness={0.2} /></mesh>
      {[0, 2 * Math.PI / 3, 4 * Math.PI / 3].map((angle) => <mesh key={angle} position={[Math.sin(angle) * 0.48, Math.cos(angle) * 0.48, 0]} rotation-z={-angle} castShadow><boxGeometry args={[0.09, 0.95, 0.045]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#e1ded2" roughness={0.46} /></mesh>)}
    </group>
  </group>
}

function BusDepotModel() {
  return <group>
    <mesh position-y={0.08} receiveShadow><boxGeometry args={[1.94, 0.16, 1.94]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#77766d" /></mesh>
    <mesh position={[0, 0.42, -0.28]} castShadow receiveShadow><boxGeometry args={[1.28, 0.68, 0.75]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#83796b" /></mesh>
    <mesh position={[0, 0.8, -0.28]} castShadow><boxGeometry args={[1.38, 0.08, 0.84]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#565b56" /></mesh>
    <mesh position={[0, 0.27, 0.52]} castShadow><boxGeometry args={[0.95, 0.32, 0.38]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#a66c52" /></mesh>
    <mesh position={[0.31, 0.42, 0.72]}><boxGeometry args={[0.28, 0.13, 0.025]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#a8bfbc" /></mesh>
    {[-0.31, 0.31].map((x) => <mesh key={x} position={[x, 0.12, 0.58]} rotation={[Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}><cylinderGeometry args={[0.09, 0.09, 0.06, 10]} /><meshStandardMaterial color="#343735" /></mesh>)}
  </group>
}

const AssetModel = memo(function AssetModel({ itemId }: { itemId: ItemId }) {
  switch (itemId) {
    case "residential": return <HouseModel />
    case "office": return <OfficeModel />
    case "road": return <RoadModel />
    case "metro": return <MetroModel />
    case "park": return <ParkModel />
    case "solar": return <SolarModel />
    case "industry": return <IndustryModel />
    case "water": return <WaterModel />
    case "school": return <SchoolModel />
    case "hospital": return <HospitalModel />
    case "wastewater": return <WastewaterModel />
    case "datacenter": return <DataCenterModel />
    case "hotel": return <HotelModel />
    case "mall": return <MallModel />
    case "landfill": return <LandfillModel />
    case "urbanforest": return <UrbanForestModel />
    case "wind": return <WindModel />
    case "busdepot": return <BusDepotModel />
  }
})

const SINGLE_CELL_MODELS = new Set(["residential", "office", "road", "metro", "park", "solar"])

function AssetHighlight({ size, color }: { size: number; color: string }) {
  const thickness = Math.max(0.055, size * 0.035)
  const y = 0.2
  return (
    <group>
      <mesh position={[0, 0.105, 0]} rotation-x={-Math.PI / 2} renderOrder={2}>
        <planeGeometry args={[size - 0.08, size - 0.08]} />
        <meshBasicMaterial color={color} transparent opacity={0.14} depthWrite={false} />
      </mesh>
      {[
        [0, y, -size / 2 + thickness / 2, size, thickness],
        [0, y, size / 2 - thickness / 2, size, thickness],
        [-size / 2 + thickness / 2, y, 0, thickness, size],
        [size / 2 - thickness / 2, y, 0, thickness, size],
      ].map(([x, barY, z, width, depth], index) => (
        <mesh key={index} position={[x, barY, z]} renderOrder={3}>
          <boxGeometry args={[width, thickness * 0.55, depth]} />
          <meshBasicMaterial color="#ffe39b" toneMapped={false} />
        </mesh>
      ))}
    </group>
  )
}

function Buildings({ city: sceneCity }: { city?: City }) {
  const storedCity = useCityStore((state) => state.city)
  const selectedItem = useCityStore((state) => state.selectedItem)
  const tool = useCityStore((state) => state.tool)
  const city = sceneCity ?? storedCity
  const assets = useMemo(() => listAssets(city), [city])
  return <>{assets.map(({ itemId, row, col }) => {
    const footprint = ITEMS[itemId].footprint
    const modelScale = footprint / (SINGLE_CELL_MODELS.has(itemId) ? 1 : 2)
    const centerX = col - GRID_SIZE / 2 + footprint / 2
    const centerZ = row - GRID_SIZE / 2 + footprint / 2
    return (
      <group key={`${row}-${col}-${itemId}`} position={[centerX, 0, centerZ]}>
        {tool === "place" && selectedItem === itemId ? <AssetHighlight size={footprint} color={ITEMS[itemId].color} /> : null}
        <group scale={modelScale}>
          <AssetModel itemId={itemId} />
        </group>
      </group>
    )
  })}</>
}

function Ground({ onHover, paintDrag, city: sceneCity, interactive = true }: {
  onHover: (cell: { row: number; col: number; item: string | null } | null) => void
  paintDrag: boolean
  city?: City
  interactive?: boolean
}) {
  const storedCity = useCityStore((state) => state.city)
  const city = sceneCity ?? storedCity
  const selectedItem = useCityStore((state) => state.selectedItem)
  const tool = useCityStore((state) => state.tool)
  const placeAt = useCityStore((state) => state.placeAt)
  const eraseAt = useCityStore((state) => state.eraseAt)
  const isDraggingAsset = useRef(false)
  const lastPaintedCell = useRef<string | null>(null)
  const lastHoveredCell = useRef<string | null>(null)
  const texture = useMemo(() => {
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas")
    const cellPixels = 32
    canvas.width = canvas.height = GRID_SIZE * cellPixels
    const context = canvas.getContext("2d")!
    for (let row = 0; row < GRID_SIZE; row += 1) {
      for (let col = 0; col < GRID_SIZE; col += 1) {
        context.fillStyle = (row + col) % 2 ? "#20211e" : "#252621"
        context.fillRect(col * cellPixels, row * cellPixels, cellPixels, cellPixels)
      }
    }
    context.strokeStyle = "#47473f"
    context.lineWidth = 1
    for (let index = 0; index <= GRID_SIZE; index += 1) {
      context.beginPath(); context.moveTo(index * cellPixels, 0); context.lineTo(index * cellPixels, canvas.height); context.stroke()
      context.beginPath(); context.moveTo(0, index * cellPixels); context.lineTo(canvas.width, index * cellPixels); context.stroke()
    }
    const map = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas)
    map.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace
    map.anisotropy = 2
    return map
  }, [])

  const toCell = (point: THREE.Vector3) => ({
    col: Math.floor(point.x + GRID_SIZE / 2),
    row: Math.floor(point.z + GRID_SIZE / 2),
  })
  const updateHover = (point: THREE.Vector3) => {
    const { row, col } = toCell(point)
    const item = city[row]?.[col]
    const key = `${row},${col}:${item ? String(item).split("@")[0] : ""}`
    if (lastHoveredCell.current === key) return
    lastHoveredCell.current = key
    onHover({ row, col, item: item ? String(item).split("@")[0] : null })
  }
  const clearHover = () => {
    if (lastHoveredCell.current === null) return
    lastHoveredCell.current = null
    onHover(null)
  }
  const applyToolAt = (row: number, col: number) => {
    const key = `${row},${col}`
    if (lastPaintedCell.current === key) return
    lastPaintedCell.current = key
    if (tool === "erase") eraseAt(row, col)
    else placeAt(row, col)
  }

  return (
    <mesh
      rotation-x={-Math.PI / 2}
      receiveShadow
      onPointerMove={interactive ? (event) => {
        event.stopPropagation()
        updateHover(event.point)
        if (paintDrag && isDraggingAsset.current && (event.nativeEvent.buttons & 1) === 1) {
          const { row, col } = toCell(event.point)
          applyToolAt(row, col)
        }
      } : undefined}
      onPointerDown={interactive ? (event) => {
        if (!paintDrag || event.nativeEvent.button !== 0) return
        event.stopPropagation()
        event.nativeEvent.preventDefault()
        isDraggingAsset.current = true
        lastPaintedCell.current = null
        const canvas = event.nativeEvent.currentTarget as HTMLCanvasElement | null
        canvas?.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId)
        const { row, col } = toCell(event.point)
        applyToolAt(row, col)
      } : undefined}
      onPointerUp={interactive ? (event) => {
        if (!isDraggingAsset.current) return
        isDraggingAsset.current = false
        const canvas = event.nativeEvent.currentTarget as HTMLCanvasElement | null
        if (canvas?.hasPointerCapture(event.pointerId)) canvas.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId)
      } : undefined}
      onPointerCancel={interactive ? () => { isDraggingAsset.current = false } : undefined}
      onPointerOut={interactive ? clearHover : undefined}
      onClick={interactive ? (event) => {
        if (event.delta > 5) return
        if (paintDrag) return
        const { row, col } = toCell(event.point)
        if (tool === "erase") eraseAt(row, col)
        else placeAt(row, col)
      } : undefined}
      onContextMenu={interactive ? (event) => {
        event.stopPropagation()
        event.nativeEvent.preventDefault()
        const { row, col } = toCell(event.point)
        eraseAt(row, col)
      } : undefined}
    >
      <planeGeometry args={[GRID_SIZE, GRID_SIZE]} />
      <meshStandardMaterial map={texture} roughness={0.95} />
    </mesh>
  )
}

function PlacementPreview({ hover }: { hover: { row: number; col: number } | null }) {
  const city = useCityStore((state) => state.city)
  const selectedItem = useCityStore((state) => state.selectedItem)
  const tool = useCityStore((state) => state.tool)
  if (!hover || tool === "erase") return null

  const size = ITEMS[selectedItem].footprint
  const cells = Array.from({ length: size * size }, (_, index) => ({
    row: hover.row + Math.floor(index / size),
    col: hover.col + index % size,
  }))
  const valid = cells.every(({ row, col }) => row >= 0 && col >= 0 && row < GRID_SIZE && col < GRID_SIZE && city[row][col] === null)
  const centerX = hover.col - GRID_SIZE / 2 + size / 2
  const centerZ = hover.row - GRID_SIZE / 2 + size / 2

  return (
    <group position={[centerX, 0, centerZ]}>
      <mesh position-y={0.018} rotation-x={-Math.PI / 2}>
        <planeGeometry args={[size * 0.97, size * 0.97]} />
        <meshBasicMaterial color={valid ? ITEMS[selectedItem].color : "#b9685f"} transparent opacity={0.34} depthWrite={false} />
      </mesh>
      <mesh position-y={0.035}>
        <boxGeometry args={[size * 0.8, 0.035, size * 0.8]} />
        <meshBasicMaterial color={valid ? ITEMS[selectedItem].color : "#b9685f"} transparent opacity={0.28} depthWrite={false} />
      </mesh>
    </group>
  )
}

export default function CityScene({ onHover = () => {}, hover = null, paintDrag = false, city, readonly = false }: {
  onHover?: (cell: { row: number; col: number; item: string | null } | null) => void
  hover?: { row: number; col: number } | null
  paintDrag?: boolean
  city?: City
  readonly?: boolean
}) {
  return (
    <Canvas frameloop="demand" camera={{ position: readonly ? [43, 48, 43] : [36, 40, 42], fov: readonly ? 48 : 42 }} dpr={[1, 1.25]}>
      <color attach="background" args={["#191a17"]} />
      <fog attach="fog" args={["#191a17", 68, 125]} />
      <hemisphereLight args={["#e5dfd2", "#343630", 1.05]} />
      <directionalLight position={[16, 44, 14]} intensity={2.1} />
      <Ground city={city} interactive={!readonly} onHover={onHover} paintDrag={paintDrag} />
      {!readonly ? <PlacementPreview hover={hover} /> : null}
      <Buildings city={city} />
      {!readonly ? <OrbitControls enabled={!paintDrag} makeDefault maxPolarAngle={Math.PI / 2.15} minDistance={8} maxDistance={88} /> : null}
    </Canvas>
  )
}


================================================================
FILE: src\components\CompareView.tsx

import {
  ArrowRight,
  CheckCircle2,
  GitCompareArrows,
} from "lucide-react"
import { lazy, Suspense } from "react"
import { computeCarbon, MODEL_HORIZON, type City } from "../lib/carbon"
import { listAssets } from "../lib/grid"
import { useCityStore } from "../store/useCityStore"
const CityScene = lazy(() => import("./CityScene"))

function LayoutCard({
  city,
  label,
  emptyMessage,
}: {
  city: City | null
  label: string
  emptyMessage: string
}) {
  const result = city ? computeCarbon(city, MODEL_HORIZON) : null
  const empty = !city
  return (
    <div className="w-full min-w-0 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-raised/40 p-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow">Scenario</div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold text-white">
            {label}
          </div>
        </div>
        {empty ? <span className="tag">No result yet</span> : <CheckCircle2 className="text-emerald" size={20} />}
      </div>
      {empty ? (
        <div className="mt-5 flex min-h-64 flex-1 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-line-strong bg-canvas/35 p-6 text-center">
          <p className="max-w-xs text-sm leading-relaxed text-muted">{emptyMessage}</p>
        </div>
      ) : <>
      <div aria-label={`${label} 3D city preview`} className="relative mt-4 aspect-[4/3] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line bg-[#191a17]" role="img">
        <Suspense fallback={<div aria-live="polite" className="grid h-full place-items-center text-sm text-muted">Preparing city preview…</div>}>
          <CityScene city={city} readonly />
        </Suspense>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2 text-[10px] text-muted">3D preview of asset placement and density</div>
      <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-line bg-canvas/55 p-5">
        <div className="text-xs text-muted">Total carbon · {MODEL_HORIZON} years</div>
        <div className="mt-1 text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white tabular-nums">
          {Math.round(result!.total).toLocaleString()}
          <span className="ml-2 text-sm font-medium text-muted">tCO₂</span>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-1 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-dim">
          estimate
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
        {[
          ["Embodied", result!.embodied, "tCO₂"],
          ["Operations + transport", result!.operational + result!.transport, "tCO₂"],
          ["Offset", result!.offset, "tCO₂"],
          ["Capital cost", result!.cost, "₹ Cr"],
        ].map(([name, value, unit]) => (
          <div
            className="rounded-xl border border-line bg-panel/50 p-3"
            key={String(name)}
          >
            <div className="text-[10px] text-muted">{name}</div>
            <div className="mt-1 text-lg font-semibold text-primary tabular-nums">
              {Math.round(Number(value)).toLocaleString()}{" "}
              <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">{unit}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      </>}
    </div>
  )
}

export default function CompareView() {
  const city = useCityStore((state) => state.city)
  const savedLayoutA = useCityStore((state) => state.savedLayoutA)
  const savedLayoutB = useCityStore((state) => state.savedLayoutB)
  const reductionResult = useCityStore((state) => state.reductionResult)
  const optimizationStatus = useCityStore((state) => state.optimizationStatus)
  const view1 = savedLayoutA ?? city
  const resultA = computeCarbon(view1, MODEL_HORIZON)
  const resultB = savedLayoutB ? computeCarbon(savedLayoutB, MODEL_HORIZON) : null
  const changePercent = resultB && resultA.total !== 0
    ? ((resultB.total - resultA.total) / Math.abs(resultA.total)) * 100
    : 0
  const relocatedCount = savedLayoutB ? (() => {
    const countByType = (layout: City) => {
      const counts = new Map<string, number>()
      for (const { itemId } of listAssets(layout)) counts.set(itemId, (counts.get(itemId) ?? 0) + 1)
      return counts
    }
    const original = countByType(view1)
    const optimized = countByType(savedLayoutB)
    const exact = new Set(listAssets(view1).map(({ itemId, row, col }) => `${itemId}:${row},${col}`))
    const samePositions = listAssets(savedLayoutB).filter(({ itemId, row, col }) => exact.has(`${itemId}:${row},${col}`)).length
    const originalCount = [...original.values()].reduce((sum, count) => sum + count, 0)
    const retainedCount = Math.min(originalCount, [...optimized.values()].reduce((sum, count) => sum + count, 0))
    return Math.max(0, retainedCount - samePositions)
  })() : 0

  return (
    <section className="panel flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-auto p-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-semibold text-white">
            <GitCompareArrows className="text-accent" size={20} />
            Compare city versions
          </div>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted">
            View 1 updates as you edit. View 2 shows the optimized city. Existing buildings may move, but none are removed.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="my-5 grid min-w-0 flex-1 grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_auto_minmax(0,1fr)] items-stretch gap-4">
        <LayoutCard city={view1} label="View 1 · Your city" emptyMessage="Your city and edits appear here." />
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center text-dim">
          <ArrowRight size={22} />
        </div>
        <LayoutCard city={savedLayoutB} label="View 2 · Optimized" emptyMessage="Run Optimize to see the updated city here." />
      </div>

      <div
        className={`mt-auto rounded-2xl border p-5 text-center ${
          !resultB ? "border-line bg-raised/30" : changePercent <= 0
            ? "border-emerald/30 bg-emerald/10"
            : "border-danger/30 bg-danger/10"
        }`}
      >
        <div
          className={`text-4xl font-extrabold tracking-tight ${
          !resultB ? "text-muted" : changePercent <= 0 ? "text-emerald" : "text-danger-soft"
          }`}
        >
          {optimizationStatus === "running" ? "Optimizing city…" : !resultB ? "No result yet" : changePercent <= 0 ? `${Math.round(-changePercent)}% lower carbon` : `${Math.round(changePercent)}% higher emissions`}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-secondary">
          {!resultB ? "Your original and optimized cities will appear side by side." : `${relocatedCount} existing assets moved. No buildings removed. ${reductionResult ? reductionResult.mode === "minimum" ? `Lowest result found: ${reductionResult.achievedPercent.toFixed(1)}% reduction. ` : `Target: ${reductionResult.targetPercent}%. Achieved: ${reductionResult.achievedPercent.toFixed(1)}%. ${reductionResult.targetMet ? "Target reached." : "Target not reached. Space, asset limits, or model values prevent further reductions."} ` : ""}Estimate over ${MODEL_HORIZON} years.`}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}


================================================================
FILE: src\components\Layout.tsx

import { useMemo, useState } from "react"
import { AlertTriangle, Lightbulb, TrendingDown } from "lucide-react"
import { computeCarbon, MODEL_HORIZON } from "../lib/carbon"
import { ITEMS } from "../data/items"
import { listAssets } from "../lib/grid"
import { useCityStore } from "../store/useCityStore"
import CanvasPlaceholder from "./CanvasPlaceholder"
import CompareView from "./CompareView"
import Palette from "./Palette"
import SourcesModal from "./SourcesModal"
import StatsPanel from "./StatsPanel"
import TimeSlider from "./TimeSlider"
import TopBar from "./TopBar"

export default function Layout() {
  const [compareMode, setCompareMode] = useState(false)
  const [sourcesOpen, setSourcesOpen] = useState(false)
  const [hoveredCell, setHoveredCell] = useState<{
    row: number
    col: number
    item: string | null
  } | null>(null)
  const city = useCityStore((state) => state.city)
  const year = useCityStore((state) => state.year)
  const savedLayoutA = useCityStore((state) => state.savedLayoutA)
  const savedLayoutB = useCityStore((state) => state.savedLayoutB)
  const reductionResult = useCityStore((state) => state.reductionResult)

  const optimizationNotice = useMemo(() => {
    if (!reductionResult || (reductionResult.mode === "target" && reductionResult.targetMet) || !savedLayoutB) return null
    const baseline = savedLayoutA ?? city
    const beforeCounts = new Map<string, number>()
    const afterCounts = new Map<string, number>()
    const beforeAssets = listAssets(baseline)
    const afterAssets = listAssets(savedLayoutB)
    for (const { itemId } of beforeAssets) beforeCounts.set(itemId, (beforeCounts.get(itemId) ?? 0) + 1)
    for (const { itemId } of afterAssets) afterCounts.set(itemId, (afterCounts.get(itemId) ?? 0) + 1)
    const beforeAnchors = new Set(beforeAssets.map(({ itemId, row, col }) => `${itemId}:${row},${col}`))
    const unchangedCount = afterAssets.filter(({ itemId, row, col }) => beforeAnchors.has(`${itemId}:${row},${col}`)).length
    const retainedCount = Math.min(beforeAssets.length, afterAssets.length)
    const relocatedCount = Math.max(0, retainedCount - unchangedCount)
    const additions = [...afterCounts].flatMap(([itemId, count]) => {
      const added = count - (beforeCounts.get(itemId) ?? 0)
      return added > 0 ? [`${ITEMS[itemId as keyof typeof ITEMS].label} +${added}`] : []
    })
    const minimumTotal = computeCarbon(savedLayoutB, MODEL_HORIZON).total
    return { additions, minimumTotal, relocatedCount }
  }, [city, reductionResult, savedLayoutA, savedLayoutB])

  const result = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      return { metrics: computeCarbon(city, year), error: null }
    } catch (error) {
      return {
        metrics: null,
        error:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : "The carbon estimate could not be calculated.",
      }
    }
  }, [city, year])

  return (
    <main className="flex h-screen min-h-[720px] flex-col overflow-hidden bg-canvas text-primary">
      <TopBar
        compareMode={compareMode}
        metrics={result.metrics}
        onCompareChange={setCompareMode}
        onOpenSources={() => setSourcesOpen(true)}
      />

      {optimizationNotice && reductionResult ? (
        <section aria-live="polite" className="mx-3 mt-2 rounded-xl border border-amber/30 bg-amber/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-primary" role={reductionResult.mode === "target" ? "alert" : "status"}>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            {reductionResult.mode === "minimum" ? <TrendingDown className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-accent" size={18} /> : <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-amber" size={18} />}
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <strong className="text-white">
                {reductionResult.mode === "minimum" ? "Lowest carbon result found with the current model" : `Target not reached: requested ${reductionResult.targetPercent}%, best result ${reductionResult.achievedPercent.toFixed(1)}%`}
              </strong>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-secondary">
                {reductionResult.mode === "minimum"
                  ? `The city is now at ${Math.round(optimizationNotice.minimumTotal).toLocaleString()} tCO₂e over ${MODEL_HORIZON} years, a ${reductionResult.achievedPercent.toFixed(1)}% reduction from the starting layout. Existing buildings are retained.`
                  : `The best layout reduces emissions by ${reductionResult.achievedPercent.toFixed(1)}%, leaving ${Math.max(0, reductionResult.targetPercent - reductionResult.achievedPercent).toFixed(1)} percentage points to go. The optimizer retained every existing building; remaining emissions include sources the current asset mix cannot directly change.`}
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-secondary"><span className="font-semibold text-white">Route used:</span> {optimizationNotice.additions.length ? `${optimizationNotice.additions.join(" · ")} · ` : "No new assets · "}{optimizationNotice.relocatedCount} existing assets repositioned.</p>
              {reductionResult.mode === "target" ? <p className="mt-2 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-secondary"><Lightbulb className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-accent" size={14} /><span>To target further, model efficiency or direct-emissions upgrades for high-emitting facilities, expand clean-energy options or their limits, or revise the city assumptions in Sources. The current model caps added wind at one turbine per run.</span></p> : <p className="mt-2 text-[10px] text-muted">“Minimum” means the lowest result found within the current grid, asset limits, and carbon assumptions.</p>}
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>
      ) : null}

      {result.error ? (
        <div className="mx-4 mt-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-danger/40 bg-danger/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-danger-soft">
          <AlertTriangle size={18} aria-hidden="true" />
          <div>
            <strong>Carbon model temporarily unavailable.</strong> Your city is
            safe. {result.error}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 gap-3 p-3">
        <Palette />
        {compareMode ? (
          <CompareView />
        ) : (
          <>
            <CanvasPlaceholder
              hoveredCell={hoveredCell}
              onHoverCell={setHoveredCell}
            />
            {result.metrics ? <StatsPanel metrics={result.metrics} /> : null}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      <TimeSlider />
      <SourcesModal open={sourcesOpen} onClose={() => setSourcesOpen(false)} />
    </main>
  )
}

import { Eraser, Keyboard, House, Building2, Route, TrainTrack, Trees, Sun, Factory, Waves, School, Hospital, Database, Droplets, Hotel, Store, Trash2, TreePine, Wind, BusFront, type LucideIcon } from "lucide-react"
import { ITEM_IDS, ITEMS, ITEM_SHORTCUTS, type ItemId } from "../data/items"
import { useCityStore } from "../store/useCityStore"

const ASSET_ICONS: Record<ItemId, LucideIcon> = {
  residential: House,
  office: Building2,
  road: Route,
  metro: TrainTrack,
  park: Trees,
  solar: Sun,
  industry: Factory,
  water: Waves,
  school: School,
  hospital: Hospital,
  wastewater: Droplets,
  datacenter: Database,
  hotel: Hotel,
  mall: Store,
  landfill: Trash2,
  urbanforest: TreePine,
  wind: Wind,
  busdepot: BusFront,
}

export default function Palette() {
  const selectedItem = useCityStore((state) => state.selectedItem)
  const tool = useCityStore((state) => state.tool)
  const setSelectedItem = useCityStore((state) => state.setSelectedItem)
  const setTool = useCityStore((state) => state.setTool)

  return (
    <aside className="panel flex min-h-0 w-[244px] shrink-0 flex-col overflow-y-auto p-3">
      <div className="mb-3 flex items-end justify-between px-1">
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow">Build palette</div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-white">
            Choose a city asset
          </div>
        </div>
        <span className="rounded-md bg-accent/10 px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold text-accent">
          {ITEM_IDS.length} assets
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        {ITEM_IDS.map((itemId) => {
          const item = ITEMS[itemId]
          const Icon = ASSET_ICONS[itemId]
          const selected = tool === "place" && selectedItem === itemId
          return (
            <button
              aria-pressed={selected}
              className={`group relative rounded-xl border p-2 text-left transition duration-200 ${
                selected
                  ? "border-accent bg-accent/10 shadow-[0_0_0_1px_var(--accent)]"
                  : "border-line bg-raised/50 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-line-strong hover:bg-raised"
              }`}
              key={itemId}
              onClick={() => setSelectedItem(itemId)}
              title={`Select ${item.label}${ITEM_SHORTCUTS[itemId] ? ` (${ITEM_SHORTCUTS[itemId]})` : ""}`}
              type="button"
            >
              <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
                <span
                  className="flex size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-md"
                  style={{ color: item.color, backgroundColor: `${item.color}22` }}
                ><Icon size={15} aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                {ITEM_SHORTCUTS[itemId] ? <kbd className="text-[9px] text-dim">{ITEM_SHORTCUTS[itemId]}</kbd> : null}
              </div>
              <div className="min-h-8 text-[11px] font-semibold leading-tight text-primary">
                {item.label}
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                <span className="tag">₹{item.cost}Cr</span>
                <span className="tag" title={item.embodied === 0 ? "Construction carbon is not modeled for this asset" : "Embodied carbon per asset, tCO₂e"}>{item.embodied === 0 ? "Embodied n/a" : `${item.embodied}t`}</span>
                <span className="tag" title="Grid footprint">{item.footprint}×{item.footprint}</span>
              </div>
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>

      <button
        aria-pressed={tool === "erase"}
        className={`mt-2 flex items-center justify-between rounded-xl border p-3 text-xs font-semibold transition ${
          tool === "erase"
            ? "border-danger bg-danger/10 text-danger-soft"
            : "border-line bg-raised/50 text-secondary hover:border-line-strong"
        }`}
        onClick={() => setTool("erase")}
        title="Erase placed assets (E)"
        type="button"
      >
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Eraser size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
          Eraser
        </span>
        <kbd className="text-[10px] text-dim">E</kbd>
      </button>

      <div className="mt-auto rounded-xl border border-line bg-canvas/50 p-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
          <Keyboard size={13} aria-hidden="true" />
          Shortcuts
        </div>
        <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-dim">
          Press 1 to 9 to select the first nine assets. Use the palette for the rest. Press E to erase.
        </p>
      </div>
    </aside>
  )
}

FILE: src\components\ScoreBadge.tsx
================================================================

import { Leaf } from "lucide-react"
import type { CarbonResult } from "../lib/carbon"
import { computeCarbon, getPopulation, MODEL_HORIZON } from "../lib/carbon"
import { useMemo } from "react"
import { useCityStore } from "../store/useCityStore"

function getGrade(annualPerResident: number) {
  if (annualPerResident < 0) return "A+"
  if (annualPerResident <= 2) return "A"
  if (annualPerResident <= 4) return "B+"
  if (annualPerResident <= 6) return "B"
  if (annualPerResident <= 10) return "C"
  if (annualPerResident <= 15) return "D"
  return "E"
}

export default function ScoreBadge({
  metrics,
  year,
}: {
  metrics: CarbonResult | null
  year: number
}) {
  const city = useCityStore((state) => state.city)
  const population = getPopulation(city)
  const gradeMetrics = useMemo(() => computeCarbon(city, MODEL_HORIZON), [city])
  const annualPerResident = metrics && population > 0
    ? gradeMetrics.total / population / MODEL_HORIZON
    : null
  const grade = annualPerResident === null ? "N/A" : getGrade(annualPerResident)
  return (
    <div aria-label={annualPerResident === null ? "Add homes to see a per resident carbon grade" : `Grade ${grade}: ${annualPerResident.toFixed(1)} tonnes of carbon per resident per year`} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border border-emerald/25 bg-emerald/10 px-3 py-2" title={`Displayed total covers ${year} years. Grade uses the average carbon per resident over 30 years.`}>
      <div className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-emerald text-sm font-extrabold text-emerald-ink">
        {grade}
      </div>
      <div className="leading-tight">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white">
          <Leaf size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
          {metrics
            ? `${Math.round(metrics.total).toLocaleString()} tCO₂`
            : "Estimate unavailable"}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-muted">
          {annualPerResident === null ? "Add residents for a grade" : `${annualPerResident.toFixed(1)} tCO₂ / resident / year · 30y avg`}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { Search, X } from "lucide-react"
import { useState } from "react"
import { CARBON_SOURCE_INPUTS, type CityItem } from "../data/items"

type Row = {
  id: string
  name: string
  value: number
  unit: string
  status: CityItem["sourceStatus"]
  basis: string
}

type Group = {
  url: string
  ref: string
  relatedRef?: string
  relatedUrl?: string
  rows: Row[]
}

const labels: Record<string, string> = {
