  "busdepot.passengers": "Average bus passenger load",
  "busdepot.kgPerPassengerKm": "Intracity bus emissions factor",
  "busdepot.cost": "Bus depot capital cost",
}

const groupMap = new Map<string, Group>()
const unsourced: Row[] = []

Object.entries(CARBON_SOURCE_INPUTS).forEach(([id, source]) => {
  const row: Row = {
    id,
    name: labels[id] ?? id,
    value: source.value,
    unit: source.unit,
    status: source.status,
    basis: source.ref || "Assumption: no source found yet.",
  }
  if (source.url) {
    const group = groupMap.get(source.url) ?? {
      url: source.url,
      ref: source.ref,
      relatedRef: source.relatedRef,
      relatedUrl: source.relatedUrl,
      rows: [],
    }
    group.rows.push(row)
    groupMap.set(source.url, group)
  } else {
    unsourced.push(row)
  }
})

const groups = Array.from(groupMap.values())
const sourcedCount = groups.reduce((total, group) => total + group.rows.length, 0)

const statusStyles: Record<CityItem["sourceStatus"], string> = {
  cited: "bg-emerald/15 text-emerald",
  candidate: "bg-amber/15 text-amber",
  assumption: "bg-accent/15 text-accent",
  placeholder: "bg-raised text-muted",
}

function host(url: string) {
  try {
    return new URL(url).hostname.replace(/^www\./, "")
  } catch {
    return url
  }
}

function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: CityItem["sourceStatus"] }) {
  return (
    <strong
      className={`rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[9px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide ${statusStyles[status]}`}
    >
      {status}
    </strong>
  )
}

export default function SourcesModal({
  open,
  onClose,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onClose: () => void
}) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("")
  if (!open) return null

  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase()
  const visibleGroups = q
    ? groups.filter((group) =>
        `${group.ref} ${group.url} ${group.rows.map((row) => row.name).join(" ")}`
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(q),
      )
    : groups
  const visibleUnsourced = q
    ? unsourced.filter((row) =>
        `${row.name} ${row.basis}`.toLowerCase().includes(q),
      )
    : unsourced

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-overlay p-6 backdrop-blur-sm"
      onMouseDown={onClose}
    >
      <section
        aria-labelledby="sources-title"
        aria-modal="true"
        className="w-full max-w-3xl rounded-2xl border border-line-strong bg-panel-solid p-5 shadow-2xl"
        onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
        role="dialog"
      >
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="eyebrow">Transparent assumptions</div>
            <div
              className="mt-1 text-xl font-semibold text-white"
              id="sources-title"
            >
              Where our numbers come from
            </div>
            <p className="mt-1 max-w-2xl text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted">
              This is a curated list of the sources we used to build the model, along with the assumptions we made where no source was available. We welcome feedback and suggestions for better sources.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] text-dim">
              {groups.length} source links · {sourcedCount} sourced inputs ·{" "}
              {unsourced.length} assumptions without a source
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            aria-label="Close sources"
            className="icon-button"
            onClick={onClose}
            title="Close sources"
            type="button"
          >
            <X size={17} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-3">
          <label className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-raised/60 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-muted">
            <Search size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
            <input
              aria-label="Search sources"
              className="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-transparent text-primary outline-none placeholder:text-dim"
              onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search by asset or source"
              value={query}
            />
          </label>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3 max-h-[58vh] overflow-auto rounded-xl border border-line">
          <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 bg-raised px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
            Source links ({visibleGroups.length})
          </div>
          {visibleGroups.map((group) => (
            <div
              className="border-t border-line px-4 py-3 text-xs text-secondary"
              key={group.url}
            >
              <a
                className="font-medium text-accent underline underline-offset-2"
                href={group.url}
                rel="noreferrer"
                target="_blank"
              >
                Open source ↗ {host(group.url)}
              </a>
              <p className="mt-1 leading-relaxed">{group.ref}</p>
              {group.relatedRef && group.relatedUrl ? (
                <p className="mt-1 leading-relaxed text-muted">
                  {group.relatedRef}{" "}
                  <a
                    className="text-accent underline underline-offset-2"
                    href={group.relatedUrl}
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    target="_blank"
                  >
                    Regional context ↗
                  </a>
                </p>
              ) : null}
              <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1">
                {group.rows.map((row) => (
                  <li
                    className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2"
                    key={row.id}
                  >
                    <StatusBadge status={row.status} />
                    <span className="text-primary">{row.name}</span>
                    <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-white">
                      {row.value.toLocaleString("en-IN", {
                        maximumFractionDigits: 3,
                      })}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-muted">{row.unit}</span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          ))}
          {visibleGroups.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="border-t border-line px-4 py-6 text-center text-xs text-muted">
              No source links match that search.
            </div>
          ) : null}

          <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 border-t border-line bg-raised px-4 py-2 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-muted">
            Assumptions with no source yet ({visibleUnsourced.length})
          </div>
          {visibleUnsourced.map((row) => (
            <div
              className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border-t border-line px-4 py-2 text-xs text-secondary"
              key={row.id}
            >
              <StatusBadge status={row.status} />
              <span className="font-medium text-primary">{row.name}</span>
              <span className="font-mono tabular-nums text-white">
                {row.value.toLocaleString("en-IN", { maximumFractionDigits: 3 })}
              </span>
              <span className="text-muted">{row.unit}</span>
              <span className="text-muted">· {row.basis}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================
FILE: src\components\StatsPanel.tsx

import { ArrowDownRight, ArrowUpRight, CircleMinus, Info } from "lucide-react"
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react"
import type { CarbonResult } from "../lib/carbon"
import { getPopulation, MODEL_HORIZON } from "../lib/carbon"
import { useCityStore } from "../store/useCityStore"
import AnimatedNumber from "./AnimatedNumber"
import CarbonChart from "./CarbonChart"

type MetricCardProps = {
  label: string
  value: number
  previous: number
  unit: string
  prefix?: string
  decimals?: number
  goodWhenDown?: boolean
}

function MetricCard({
  label,
  value,
  previous,
  unit,
  prefix,
  decimals,
  goodWhenDown = true,
}: MetricCardProps) {
  const difference = value - previous
  const improved = goodWhenDown ? difference < 0 : difference > 0
  const DeltaIcon =
    difference === 0
      ? CircleMinus
      : difference > 0
        ? ArrowUpRight
        : ArrowDownRight
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-line bg-raised/45 p-3">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <span className="text-[10px] font-medium text-muted">{label}</span>
        <span
          className={`flex items-center gap-0.5 text-[9px] font-semibold ${
            difference === 0
              ? "text-dim"
              : improved
                ? "text-emerald"
                : "text-danger-soft"
          }`}
        >
          <DeltaIcon size={11} aria-hidden="true" />
          {Math.abs(difference).toLocaleString("en-IN", {
            maximumFractionDigits: 0,
          })}
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1 whitespace-nowrap text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-white tabular-nums">
        <AnimatedNumber decimals={decimals} prefix={prefix} value={value} />
        <span className="ml-1 text-[10px] font-medium text-muted">{unit}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="mt-0.5 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-dim">
        estimate
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function StatsPanel({ metrics }: { metrics: CarbonResult }) {
  const city = useCityStore((state) => state.city)
  const previous = useRef(metrics)
  const comparison = previous.current
  useEffect(() => {
    previous.current = metrics
  }, [metrics])

  const population = getPopulation(city)
  const perCapita = metrics.total / Math.max(population, 100)
  const meter = Math.max(4, Math.min(96, 100 - perCapita * 4))

  const cards: MetricCardProps[] = [
    {
      label: "Total carbon",
      value: metrics.total,
      previous: comparison.total,
      unit: "tCO₂",
    },
    {
      label: "Embodied",
      value: metrics.embodied,
      previous: comparison.embodied,
      unit: "tCO₂",
    },
    {
      label: "Operations + transit",
      value: metrics.operational + metrics.transport,
      previous: comparison.operational + comparison.transport,
      unit: "tCO₂",
    },
    {
      label: "Offset",
      value: metrics.offset,
      previous: comparison.offset,
      unit: "tCO₂",
      goodWhenDown: false,
    },
    {
      label: "Capital cost",
      value: metrics.cost,
      previous: comparison.cost,
      unit: "Cr",
      prefix: "₹",
      decimals: 1,
    },
    {
      label: "Net energy",
      value: metrics.energy,
      previous: comparison.energy,
      unit: "MWh",
    },
  ]

  return (
    <aside className="panel flex w-[380px] shrink-0 flex-col overflow-hidden p-4">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow">Live impact model</div>
          <div className="mt-1 text-base font-semibold text-white">
            {MODEL_HORIZON} year city outlook
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          className="icon-button"
          title="All values are modeled estimates"
          type="button"
        >
          <Info size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
          <span className="sr-only">About model estimates</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-line bg-canvas/60 p-3">
        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-secondary">
            Carbon intensity
          </span>
          <span className="text-[10px] text-muted">
            {perCapita.toFixed(1)} tCO₂ / capita
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="carbon-meter">
          <span style={{ left: `${meter}%` }} />
        </div>
        <div className="mt-1.5 flex justify-between text-[9px] text-dim">
          <span>High impact</span>
          <span>Net zero</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        {cards.map((card) => (
          <MetricCard {...card} key={card.label} />
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <div className="text-xs font-semibold text-white">
            Carbon trajectory
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-muted">
            Total tCO₂ · years 0 to {MODEL_HORIZON}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2 text-[9px] text-muted">
          <span className="text-amber">● Embodied</span>
          <span className="text-danger-soft">● Ops</span>
          <span className="text-emerald">● Offset</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <CarbonChart city={city} />
    </aside>
  )
}


================================================================
FILE: src\components\TimeSlider.tsx
================================================================

import { Pause, Play, Sparkles, Target, TrendingDown } from "lucide-react"
import { useEffect, useState } from "react"
import { useCityStore } from "../store/useCityStore"
import { MODEL_HORIZON } from "../lib/carbon"

export default function TimeSlider() {
  const year = useCityStore((state) => state.year)
  const setYear = useCityStore((state) => state.setYear)
  const optimizeInWorker = useCityStore((state) => state.optimizeInWorker)
  const optimizationStatus = useCityStore((state) => state.optimizationStatus)
  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(false)
  const [budget, setBudget] = useState(900)
  const [targetReduction, setTargetReduction] = useState("25")
  const normalizeTargetReduction = (value: string) => {
    const parsed = value.trim() === "" ? 1 : Number(value)
    return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? Math.max(1, Math.min(99, Math.round(parsed))) : 1
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!playing) return
    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {
      const next = useCityStore.getState().year + 1
      if (next > MODEL_HORIZON) {
        setYear(0)
      } else {
        setYear(next)
      }
    }, 400)
    return () => window.clearInterval(timer)
  }, [playing, setYear])

  return (
    <footer aria-busy={optimizationStatus === "running"} className="flex h-[88px] shrink-0 items-center gap-4 border-t border-line bg-panel/85 px-5 backdrop-blur-xl">
      <button
        aria-label={
          playing ? "Pause carbon timeline" : "Play carbon timeline"
        }
        className="flex size-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-accent text-accent-ink shadow-lg shadow-accent/20 transition hover:brightness-110 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent"
        onClick={() => setPlaying((current) => !current)}
        title={playing ? "Pause carbon timeline" : "Play carbon timeline"}
        type="button"
      >
        {playing ? (
          <Pause size={17} fill="currentColor" />
        ) : (
          <Play size={17} fill="currentColor" />
        )}
      </button>
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] font-medium text-muted">
          <span>Construction</span>
          <strong className="rounded-md bg-accent/10 px-2 py-1 text-accent">
            Year {year}
          </strong>
          <span>{MODEL_HORIZON} year view</span>
        </div>
        <input
          aria-label="Projection year"
          className="timeline-slider"
          max={MODEL_HORIZON}
          min="0"
          onChange={(event) => setYear(Number(event.target.value))}
          type="range"
          value={year}
        />
      </div>
      <div className="h-10 w-px bg-line" />
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <label className="relative">
          <span className="absolute left-3 top-1.5 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-dim">
            Budget
          </span>
          <input
            aria-label="Optimization budget in crore rupees"
            className="h-11 w-36 rounded-xl border border-line bg-raised px-3 pt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white tabular-nums outline-none transition focus:border-accent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent/20"
            min="0"
            onChange={(event) => setBudget(Number(event.target.value))}
            type="number"
            value={budget}
          />
          <span className="absolute right-3 top-4 text-[10px] text-muted">
            ₹ Cr
          </span>
        </label>
        <button
          className="btn-primary h-11"
            disabled={optimizationStatus === "running"}
            onClick={() => void optimizeInWorker("budget", budget)}
          title="Optimize current city within budget"
          type="button"
        >
          <Sparkles size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
          Optimize
        </button>
        <label className="relative">
          <span className="absolute left-3 top-1.5 text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-dim">
            Target reduction
          </span>
          <input
            aria-label="Target carbon emission reduction percentage"
            className="h-11 w-28 rounded-xl border border-line bg-raised px-3 pr-8 pt-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white tabular-nums outline-none transition focus:border-accent focus:ring-2 focus:ring-accent/20"
            min="1"
            max="99"
            step="1"
            inputMode="numeric"
            onChange={(event) => setTargetReduction(event.target.value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 2))}
            onBlur={() => setTargetReduction(String(normalizeTargetReduction(targetReduction)))}
            type="text"
            value={targetReduction}
          />
          <span className="absolute right-3 top-4 text-[10px] text-muted">%</span>
        </label>
        <button
          className="btn-secondary h-11 whitespace-nowrap"
          disabled={optimizationStatus === "running"}
          onClick={() => void optimizeInWorker("reduction", normalizeTargetReduction(targetReduction))}
          title="Add cleaner assets to reach this target, or show the closest result possible"
          type="button"
        >
          <Target size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
          {optimizationStatus === "running" ? "Working…" : "Optimize target"}
        </button>
        <button
          className="btn-secondary h-11 whitespace-nowrap"
          disabled={optimizationStatus === "running"}
          onClick={() => void optimizeInWorker("minimum", 99)}
          title="Find the lowest carbon result allowed by the model and available space"
          type="button"
        >
          <TrendingDown size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
          Find minimum
        </button>
      </div>
    </footer>
  )
}


================================================================
FILE: src\components\TopBar.tsx
================================================================

import { BookOpen, RotateCcw, Route } from "lucide-react"
import { useEffect } from "react"
import type { CarbonResult } from "../lib/carbon"
import { useCityStore } from "../store/useCityStore"
import ScoreBadge from "./ScoreBadge"

type Props = {
  compareMode: boolean
  metrics: CarbonResult | null
  onCompareChange: (enabled: boolean) => void
  onOpenSources: () => void
}

export default function TopBar({
  compareMode,
  metrics,
  onCompareChange,
  onOpenSources,
}: Props) {
  useEffect(() => { document.title = "Buildit" }, [])
  const resetCity = useCityStore((state) => state.resetCity)
  const loadStarter = useCityStore((state) => state.loadStarter)
  const year = useCityStore((state) => state.year)

  return (
    <header className="flex h-[72px] shrink-0 items-center justify-between border-b border-line bg-panel/80 px-5 backdrop-blur-xl">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="brand-mark" aria-hidden="true">
          <span />
          <span />
          <span />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-emerald">
            CITY CARBON PLANNER
          </div>
          <div className="text-lg font-semibold tracking-tight text-white">
            Buildit
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <button
          className="btn-secondary"
          onClick={resetCity}
          title="Clear every tile and start again"
        >
          <RotateCcw size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
          Reset city
        </button>
        <button
          className="btn-secondary"
          onClick={loadStarter}
          title="Load the busy trial city"
        >
          <Route size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
          Load trial city
        </button>
        <button
          className="btn-secondary"
          onClick={onOpenSources}
          title="Open model sources and assumptions"
        >
          <BookOpen size={15} aria-hidden="true" />
          Sources
        </button>
        <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-line px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-secondary transition hover:border-line-strong">
          Compare
          <input
            className="peer sr-only"
            type="checkbox"
            checked={compareMode}
            onChange={(event) => onCompareChange(event.target.checked)}
          />
          <span className="relative h-5 w-9 rounded-full bg-raised transition peer-checked:bg-accent peer-focus-visible:ring-2 peer-focus-visible:ring-accent">
            <span className="absolute left-0.5 top-0.5 size-4 rounded-full bg-white transition peer-checked:translate-x-4" />
          </span>
        </label>
        <ScoreBadge metrics={metrics} year={year} />
      </div>
    </header>
  )
}

import sourceInputs from "./carbon-inputs.json" with { type: "json" }

export type ItemId =
  | "residential"
  | "office"
  | "road"
  | "metro"
  | "park"
  | "solar"
  | "industry"
  | "water"
  | "school"
  | "hospital"
  | "wastewater"
  | "datacenter"
  | "hotel"
  | "mall"
  | "landfill"
  | "urbanforest"
  | "wind"
  | "busdepot"

export type CityItem = {
  label: string
  color: string
  embodied: number
  energy: number
  directEmissions: number
  cost: number
  floor: number
  sink: number
  capacity: number
  footprint: number
  description: string
  sourceStatus: "cited" | "candidate" | "assumption" | "placeholder"
}

type InputRow = { value: number; unit: string; ref: string; url: string; relatedRef?: string; relatedUrl?: string; year: number | null; status: CityItem["sourceStatus"] }
const input = sourceInputs as Record<string, InputRow>
const value = (id: string) => input[id].value
const areaScaleFromBaseFacility = (footprint: number) => footprint ** 2 / 4

// Asset totals are derived from the supplied table: kg/m² × m² ÷ 1000 = tCO₂e;
// kWh/m²/year × m² ÷ 1000 = MWh/year. Solar's generation is negative city energy.

export const TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON = value("TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON")
export const TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO = value("TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO")
export const ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK = value("ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK")
export const CARBON_SOURCE_INPUTS = input

================================================================
FILE: src\lib\carbon.ts
================================================================

import {
  ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK,
  GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR,
  ITEMS,
  TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO,
  TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON,
  type ItemId,
} from "../data/items.ts"
import { footprintCells, GRID_SIZE, listAssets } from "./grid.ts"

export { GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR, TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON }

export type CityCell = ItemId | `${ItemId}@${number},${number}` | null
export type City = Array<Array<CityCell>>

export type CarbonResult = {
  embodied: number
  operational: number
  transport: number
  offset: number
  total: number
  cost: number
  energy: number
}

export const MODEL_HORIZON = 30

export function computeCarbon(city: City, year: number): CarbonResult {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(city) ||
    city.length !== GRID_SIZE ||
    city.some((row) => row.length !== GRID_SIZE)
  ) {
    throw new Error(`Expected a ${GRID_SIZE} × ${GRID_SIZE} city grid.`)
  }

  const years = Math.max(0, Math.min(MODEL_HORIZON, year))
  let embodied = 0
  let annualEnergy = 0
  let annualConsumedEnergy = 0
  let annualGeneratedEnergy = 0
  let annualDirectEmissions = 0
  let annualSink = 0
  let annualTransport = 0
  let cost = 0

  const assets = listAssets(city)
  const parks = assets.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "park")
  const metros = assets.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "metro")
  const parkCoverage = Array.from({ length: GRID_SIZE }, () => new Uint8Array(GRID_SIZE))
  const metroCoverage = Array.from({ length: GRID_SIZE }, () => new Uint8Array(GRID_SIZE))
  const markCoverage = (coverage: Uint8Array[], asset: (typeof assets)[number], radius: number) => {
    for (const cell of footprintCells(asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col)) {
      for (let row = Math.max(0, cell.row - radius); row <= Math.min(GRID_SIZE - 1, cell.row + radius); row += 1) {
        for (let col = Math.max(0, cell.col - radius); col <= Math.min(GRID_SIZE - 1, cell.col + radius); col += 1) {
          coverage[row][col] = 1
        }
      }
    }
  }
  parks.forEach((park) => markCoverage(parkCoverage, park, 1))
  metros.forEach((metro) => markCoverage(metroCoverage, metro, 3))
  assets.forEach(({ itemId, row, col }) => {
    const item = ITEMS[itemId]
    if (!item) throw new Error(`Unknown city item: ${itemId}`)
    embodied += item.embodied
    const cells = footprintCells(itemId, row, col)
    const nearPark = itemId !== "park" && cells.some(({ row: cellRow, col: cellCol }) => parkCoverage[cellRow][cellCol] === 1)
    const adjustedEnergy = item.energy > 0 && nearPark
      ? item.energy * ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK
      : item.energy
    annualEnergy += adjustedEnergy
    annualConsumedEnergy += Math.max(adjustedEnergy, 0)
    annualGeneratedEnergy += Math.max(-adjustedEnergy, 0)
    annualSink += item.sink
    annualDirectEmissions += item.directEmissions
    if (itemId === "residential") {
      const nearMetro = cells.some(({ row: cellRow, col: cellCol }) => metroCoverage[cellRow][cellCol] === 1)
      annualTransport += item.capacity * TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON * (nearMetro ? TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO : 1)
    }
    cost += item.cost
  })

  const operational = (annualConsumedEnergy * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR + annualDirectEmissions) * years
  const transport = annualTransport * years
  const offset = (annualSink + annualGeneratedEnergy * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR) * years

  return {
    embodied,
    operational,
    transport,
    offset,
    total: embodied + operational + transport - offset,
    cost,
    energy: annualEnergy * years,
  }
}

export function getPopulation(city: City) {
  return listAssets(city).reduce((total, { itemId }) => total + ITEMS[itemId].capacity, 0)
}


================================================================
FILE: src\lib\grid.test.ts
================================================================

import assert from "node:assert/strict"
import test from "node:test"
import { ITEM_IDS, ITEMS } from "../data/items.ts"
import { computeCarbon, getPopulation, GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR, TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON } from "./carbon.ts"
import { canPlace, eraseAsset, footprintCells, getCellItemId, GRID_SIZE, listAssets, placeAsset } from "./grid.ts"
import { optimizeCity, optimizeCityForReduction } from "./optimizer.ts"
import { useCityStore } from "../store/useCityStore.ts"

const emptyCity = () => Array.from({ length: GRID_SIZE }, () => Array(GRID_SIZE).fill(null))

test("catalog contains the supported city assets", () => {
  assert.equal(ITEM_IDS.length, 18)
  assert.deepEqual(ITEM_IDS, ["residential", "office", "road", "metro", "park", "solar", "industry", "water", "school", "hospital", "wastewater", "datacenter", "hotel", "mall", "landfill", "urbanforest", "wind", "busdepot"])
  assert.equal("mixed-use" in ITEMS, false)
  assert.equal(GRID_SIZE, 40)
})

test("assets occupy realistic one, two, and three tile footprints", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  assert.equal(placeAsset(city, "residential", 0, 0), true)
  assert.equal(city[0][0], "residential")
  for (const itemId of ["industry", "water", "school", "hospital", "wastewater", "datacenter", "mall", "busdepot"] as const) {
    const grid = emptyCity()
    assert.equal(placeAsset(grid, itemId, 4, 5), true)
    assert.equal(footprintCells(itemId, 4, 5).length, ITEMS[itemId].footprint ** 2)
    assert.equal(grid.flat().filter((cell) => getCellItemId(cell) === itemId).length, ITEMS[itemId].footprint ** 2)
    assert.equal(listAssets(grid).length, 1)
  }
})

test("multi-tile placement rejects collisions and every out-of-grid edge", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(city, "road", 1, 2)
  assert.equal(canPlace(city, "industry", 1, 1), false)
  assert.equal(placeAsset(city, "industry", 39, 39), false)
  assert.equal(placeAsset(city, "water", 39, 38), false)
})

test("erasing any footprint cell removes only that asset", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(city, "industry", 5, 5)
  placeAsset(city, "water", 5, 8)
  placeAsset(city, "road", 5, 10)
  assert.equal(eraseAsset(city, 6, 6), true)
  assert.equal(city[5][5], null)
  assert.equal(city[6][6], null)
  assert.equal(city[5][8], "water@5,8")
  assert.equal(city[6][9], "water@5,8")
  assert.equal(city[5][10], "road")
})

test("adjacent same-type footprints retain separate identities", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(city, "water", 5, 5)
  placeAsset(city, "water", 5, 7)
  assert.equal(listAssets(city).length, 2)
  eraseAsset(city, 6, 7)
  assert.equal(city[5][5], "water@5,5")
  assert.equal(city[6][6], "water@5,5")
  assert.equal(city[5][7], null)
  assert.equal(city[6][8], null)
  assert.equal(listAssets(city).length, 1)
})

test("industry and water are counted once in metrics, population, and trajectory", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(city, "industry", 2, 3)
  placeAsset(city, "water", 8, 9)
  const year0 = computeCarbon(city, 0)
  const year1 = computeCarbon(city, 1)
  assert.equal(year0.embodied, ITEMS.industry.embodied + ITEMS.water.embodied)
  assert.equal(year0.cost, ITEMS.industry.cost + ITEMS.water.cost)
  assert.equal(year1.energy, ITEMS.industry.energy + ITEMS.water.energy)
  assert.equal(year1.operational, ITEMS.industry.energy * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR + ITEMS.water.directEmissions)
  assert.equal(year1.transport, 0)
  assert.equal(getPopulation(city), ITEMS.industry.capacity + ITEMS.water.capacity)
  assert.equal(year1.total, year1.embodied + year1.operational + year1.transport - year1.offset)
  assert.equal(computeCarbon(city, 30).cost, year1.cost)
})

test("candidate assets must pass the same footprint and budget prerequisites", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  const budget = ITEMS.industry.cost - 1
  assert.equal(ITEMS.industry.cost <= budget, false)
  assert.equal(canPlace(city, "water", 18, 18), true)
  placeAsset(city, "office", 19, 19)
  assert.equal(canPlace(city, "industry", 18, 18), false)
})

test("optimizer preserves valid footprints, avoids collisions, and stays within budget", () => {
  const source = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(source, "industry", 2, 2)
  placeAsset(source, "water", 2, 4)
  placeAsset(source, "road", 2, 6)
  const budget = 100
  const sourceAssets = listAssets(source)
  const optimized = optimizeCity(source, budget)
  assert.ok(computeCarbon(optimized, 0).cost - computeCarbon(source, 0).cost <= budget)
  for (const existing of sourceAssets) {
    assert.ok(listAssets(optimized).some((asset) => asset.itemId === existing.itemId && asset.row === existing.row && asset.col === existing.col))
  }
  const validationGrid = emptyCity()
  for (const asset of listAssets(optimized)) {
    assert.equal(canPlace(validationGrid, asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col), true)
    placeAsset(validationGrid, asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col)
  }
})

test("target optimizer reaches an achievable reduction while preserving all original assets", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(city, "hotel", 5, 5)
  const original = city.map((row) => [...row])
  const optimized = optimizeCityForReduction(city, 20)
  assert.deepEqual(city, original)
  assert.ok(computeCarbon(optimized, 30).total <= computeCarbon(city, 30).total * 0.8)
  assert.ok(listAssets(optimized).some(({ itemId }) => itemId === "hotel"))
  assert.ok(listAssets(optimized).length > listAssets(city).length)
  for (const asset of listAssets(optimized)) {
    const validation = emptyCity()
    assert.equal(canPlace(validation, asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col), true)
    placeAsset(validation, asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col)
  }
})

test("optimizer can reposition an existing home near metro without removing or duplicating assets", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(city, "residential", 1, 1)
  placeAsset(city, "metro", 12, 12)
  const originalAssets = listAssets(city)
  const optimized = optimizeCityForReduction(city, 1)
  const optimizedAssets = listAssets(optimized)
  assert.equal(optimizedAssets.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "residential").length, 1)
  assert.equal(optimizedAssets.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "metro").length, 1)
  assert.ok(optimizedAssets.some(({ itemId, row, col }) => itemId === "residential" && (row !== 1 || col !== 1)))
  assert.ok(computeCarbon(optimized, 30).total < computeCarbon(city, 30).total)
  for (const itemId of new Set(originalAssets.map(({ itemId }) => itemId))) {
    assert.ok(optimizedAssets.filter((asset) => asset.itemId === itemId).length >= originalAssets.filter((asset) => asset.itemId === itemId).length)
  }
})

test("solar is sited by major electricity users and wind additions are limited", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(city, "industry", 18, 18)
  placeAsset(city, "datacenter", 22, 18)
  placeAsset(city, "datacenter", 22, 22)
  const optimized = optimizeCityForReduction(city, 80)
  const assets = listAssets(optimized)
  assert.ok(assets.some(({ itemId }) => itemId === "solar"))
  assert.ok(assets.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "wind").length <= 1)
  for (const solar of assets.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "solar")) {
    const nearbyMajorLoad = assets.some((asset) =>
      ["industry", "datacenter", "mall", "wastewater", "busdepot"].includes(asset.itemId)
      && Math.max(Math.abs(asset.row - solar.row), Math.abs(asset.col - solar.col)) <= 6,
    )
    assert.ok(nearbyMajorLoad)
  }
})

test("existing solar and forest assets are regrouped beside relevant neighborhoods", () => {
  const city = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(city, "industry", 20, 20)
  placeAsset(city, "residential", 25, 25)
  placeAsset(city, "office", 27, 25)
  placeAsset(city, "solar", 0, 0)
  placeAsset(city, "urbanforest", 0, 2)
  const optimized = optimizeCityForReduction(city, 1)
  const assets = listAssets(optimized)
  const solar = assets.find(({ itemId }) => itemId === "solar")!
  const forest = assets.find(({ itemId }) => itemId === "urbanforest")!
  assert.ok(Math.max(Math.abs(solar.row - 20), Math.abs(solar.col - 20)) <= 6)
  assert.ok(assets.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "residential" || itemId === "office").some((home) =>
    Math.max(Math.abs(forest.row - home.row), Math.abs(forest.col - home.col)) <= 6,
  ))
})

test("starter optimization moves existing buildings and never adds roads", () => {
  useCityStore.getState().loadStarter()
  const before = useCityStore.getState().city
  const original = listAssets(before)
  const originalAnchors = new Set(original.map(({ itemId, row, col }) => `${itemId}:${row},${col}`))
  const originalRoadAnchors = original.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "road").map(({ itemId, row, col }) => `${itemId}:${row},${col}`).sort()
  const originalRoads = original.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "road").length
  const optimized = optimizeCityForReduction(before, 10)
  const after = listAssets(optimized)
  const unchanged = after.filter(({ itemId, row, col }) => originalAnchors.has(`${itemId}:${row},${col}`)).length
  assert.ok(unchanged < original.length, "at least one existing building should move")
  assert.equal(after.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "road").length, originalRoads)
  assert.deepEqual(after.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "road").map(({ itemId, row, col }) => `${itemId}:${row},${col}`).sort(), originalRoadAnchors)
  assert.ok(after.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "solar").length <= original.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "solar").length + 75)
  assert.ok(after.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "wind").length <= original.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "wind").length + 1)
})

test("starter optimizers improve the city, obey the budget, and can meet a 50% target", () => {
  useCityStore.getState().loadStarter()
  const starter = useCityStore.getState().city
  const baseline = computeCarbon(starter, 30)
  const original = listAssets(starter)
  const budgetResult = optimizeCity(starter, 300)
  const budgetMetrics = computeCarbon(budgetResult, 30)
  assert.ok(budgetMetrics.cost - baseline.cost <= 300)
  assert.ok(budgetMetrics.total < baseline.total * 0.95, "the 300 Cr plan should make a meaningful reduction")
  const targetResult = optimizeCityForReduction(starter, 50)
  const targetMetrics = computeCarbon(targetResult, 30)
  assert.ok(targetMetrics.total <= baseline.total * 0.5, "target optimization should reach 50% on the starter city")
  assert.ok(listAssets(targetResult).filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "wind").length <= original.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "wind").length + 1)
  const optimizedCounts = new Map<string, number>()
  for (const { itemId } of listAssets(targetResult)) optimizedCounts.set(itemId, (optimizedCounts.get(itemId) ?? 0) + 1)
  const originalCounts = new Map<string, number>()
  for (const { itemId } of original) originalCounts.set(itemId, (originalCounts.get(itemId) ?? 0) + 1)
  for (const [itemId, count] of originalCounts) {
    assert.ok((optimizedCounts.get(itemId) ?? 0) >= count, `original ${itemId} assets should be preserved`)
  }
})

test("starter and reset layouts remain valid with the new catalog", () => {
  const store = useCityStore.getState()
  store.loadStarter()
  const starter = useCityStore.getState().city
  assert.ok(listAssets(starter).length > 0)
  assert.ok(starter.flat().every((cell) => {
    const itemId = getCellItemId(cell)
    return itemId === null || ITEMS[itemId] !== undefined
  }))
  assert.deepEqual(starter.map((row) => row.length), Array(GRID_SIZE).fill(GRID_SIZE))
  assert.deepEqual(new Set(listAssets(starter).map(({ itemId }) => itemId)), new Set(ITEM_IDS))
  const emptyCells = starter.flat().filter((cell) => cell === null).length
  assert.equal(listAssets(starter).length, 410)
  assert.ok(emptyCells > GRID_SIZE * GRID_SIZE * 0.05)
  assert.ok(emptyCells < GRID_SIZE * GRID_SIZE * 0.7, "the trial city should still feel built up")
  assert.equal(computeCarbon(starter, 30).cost > 0, true)
  useCityStore.getState().loadStarter()
  assert.notDeepEqual(useCityStore.getState().city, starter, "each starter load should randomize the asset layout")
  useCityStore.getState().resetCity()
  assert.ok(useCityStore.getState().city.flat().every((cell) => cell === null))
  assert.equal(useCityStore.getState().savedLayoutA, null)
  assert.equal(useCityStore.getState().savedLayoutB, null)
})

  useCityStore.getState().resetCity()
  useCityStore.getState().setSelectedItem("industry")
  useCityStore.getState().placeAt(3, 4)
  useCityStore.getState().saveLayout("A")

  assert.equal(computeCarbon(snapshot, 30).cost, ITEMS.industry.cost)
  useCityStore.getState().eraseAt(4, 5)
  assert.equal(computeCarbon(useCityStore.getState().savedLayoutA!, 30).cost, 0)
  assert.equal(computeCarbon(useCityStore.getState().city, 30).cost, 0)
})

test("Optimize snapshots the original into View 1 and an independent result into View 2", () => {
  const store = useCityStore.getState()
  store.resetCity()
  store.setSelectedItem("residential")
  store.placeAt(8, 8)
  const before = useCityStore.getState().city.map((row) => [...row])
  useCityStore.getState().optimize(100)

  const optimized = useCityStore.getState()
  assert.deepEqual(optimized.savedLayoutA, before)
  assert.deepEqual(optimized.savedLayoutB, optimized.city)
  assert.notEqual(optimized.savedLayoutA, optimized.savedLayoutB)
  assert.ok(computeCarbon(optimized.savedLayoutB!, 30).total < computeCarbon(optimized.savedLayoutA!, 30).total)

  const view2BeforeEdit = optimized.savedLayoutB!.map((row) => [...row])
  const openCell = optimized.city.flatMap((line, row) => line.map((cell, col) => cell === null ? { row, col } : null)).find(Boolean)!
  optimized.setSelectedItem("office")
  optimized.placeAt(openCell.row, openCell.col)
  const afterEdit = useCityStore.getState()
  assert.deepEqual(afterEdit.savedLayoutA, afterEdit.city)
  assert.deepEqual(afterEdit.savedLayoutB, view2BeforeEdit)
})

})

  useCityStore.getState().resetCity()
  useCityStore.getState().setSelectedItem("hotel")
  useCityStore.getState().placeAt(5, 5)
  const before = useCityStore.getState().city.map((row) => [...row])

})

test("metro reduces nearby residential transport and park reduces nearby building energy", () => {
  const residential = emptyCity()

  const withMetro = residential.map((row) => [...row])
  placeAsset(withMetro, "metro", 8, 8)
  assert.equal(computeCarbon(withMetro, 30).transport, 200 * 0.34 * 0.7 * 30)
  assert.equal(computeCarbon(residential, 30).transport, 200 * 0.34 * 30)

  const office = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(office, "office", 5, 5)
  const withPark = office.map((row) => [...row])

  assert.equal(computeCarbon(office, 1).energy, 1440)
  assert.ok(computeCarbon(withPark, 1).operational < computeCarbon(office, 1).operational)
})

  const city = emptyCity()
  placeAsset(city, "residential", 8, 8)
  placeAsset(city, "road", 9, 8)
  const original = city.map((row) => [...row])
  const optimized = optimizeCity(city, 100)
  assert.deepEqual(city, original)
  assert.ok(computeCarbon(optimized, 0).cost - computeCarbon(city, 0).cost <= 100)
  assert.ok(computeCarbon(optimized, 30).total < computeCarbon(city, 30).total)
  assert.ok(listAssets(optimized).length > listAssets(city).length)
  assert.deepEqual(optimizeCity(city, 0), city)
})


================================================================
FILE: src\lib\grid.ts
================================================================

import { ITEMS, type ItemId } from "../data/items.ts"
import type { City, CityCell } from "./carbon.ts"

export type Cell = { row: number; col: number }
export const GRID_SIZE = 40

export function getCellItemId(cell: CityCell): ItemId | null {
  if (!cell) return null
  return cell.includes("@") ? cell.slice(0, cell.indexOf("@")) as ItemId : cell as ItemId
}

function getCellAnchor(cell: CityCell, row: number, col: number): Cell {
  if (!cell || !cell.includes("@")) return { row, col }
  const [, anchor] = cell.split("@")
  const [anchorRow, anchorCol] = anchor.split(",").map(Number)
  return { row: anchorRow, col: anchorCol }
}

export function footprintCells(itemId: ItemId, row: number, col: number): Cell[] {
  const size = ITEMS[itemId].footprint
  return Array.from({ length: size * size }, (_, index) => ({
    row: row + Math.floor(index / size),
    col: col + (index % size),
  }))
}

export function canPlace(city: City, itemId: ItemId, row: number, col: number) {
  return footprintCells(itemId, row, col).every(
    ({ row: r, col: c }) => r >= 0 && c >= 0 && r < city.length && c < city[r].length && city[r][c] === null,
  )
}

export function placeAsset(city: City, itemId: ItemId, row: number, col: number) {
  if (!canPlace(city, itemId, row, col)) return false
  const value: CityCell = ITEMS[itemId].footprint > 1 ? `${itemId}@${row},${col}` : itemId
  footprintCells(itemId, row, col).forEach(({ row: r, col: c }) => { city[r][c] = value })
  return true
}

export function findAssetAnchor(city: City, row: number, col: number): Cell | null {
  const cell = city[row]?.[col]
  const itemId = getCellItemId(cell)
  if (!itemId) return null
  if (cell?.includes("@")) return getCellAnchor(cell, row, col)
  const size = ITEMS[itemId].footprint
  for (let r = Math.max(0, row - size + 1); r <= row; r += 1) {
    for (let c = Math.max(0, col - size + 1); c <= col; c += 1) {
      if (footprintCells(itemId, r, c).every((cell) => city[cell.row]?.[cell.col] === itemId)) return { row: r, col: c }
    }
  }
  return { row, col }
}

export function eraseAsset(city: City, row: number, col: number) {
  const cell = city[row]?.[col]
  if (!cell) return false
  const anchor = findAssetAnchor(city, row, col)
  if (!anchor) return false
  const itemId = getCellItemId(cell)!
  const value: CityCell = ITEMS[itemId].footprint > 1 ? `${itemId}@${anchor.row},${anchor.col}` : itemId
  footprintCells(itemId, anchor.row, anchor.col).forEach(({ row: r, col: c }) => {
    if (city[r]?.[c] === value) city[r][c] = null
  })
  return true
}

export function listAssets(city: City): Array<{ itemId: ItemId; row: number; col: number }> {
  const visited = new Set<string>()
  const assets: Array<{ itemId: ItemId; row: number; col: number }> = []
  city.forEach((line, row) => line.forEach((cell, col) => {
    const itemId = getCellItemId(cell)
    if (!itemId || visited.has(`${row},${col}`)) return
    const anchor = getCellAnchor(cell, row, col)
    if (anchor.row !== row || anchor.col !== col) return
    assets.push({ itemId, row, col })
    footprintCells(itemId, row, col).forEach((footprintCell) => visited.add(`${footprintCell.row},${footprintCell.col}`))
  }))
  return assets
}


================================================================
FILE: src\lib\optimizer.ts
================================================================

import { ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK, GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR, ITEMS, TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO, TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON, type ItemId } from "../data/items.ts"
import { computeCarbon, MODEL_HORIZON, type City } from "./carbon.ts"
import { canPlace, eraseAsset, footprintCells, GRID_SIZE, listAssets, placeAsset } from "./grid.ts"

const OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS = ["solar", "wind", "urbanforest", "park", "metro"] as const satisfies readonly ItemId[]
const MAX_NEW_ASSETS: Record<(typeof OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS)[number], number> = {
  solar: 75,
  wind: 1,
  urbanforest: 40,
  park: 40,
  metro: 8,
}
type Position = { row: number; col: number }

function nearTargets(itemId: ItemId, row: number, col: number, targets: ReturnType<typeof listAssets>, radius: number) {
  const size = ITEMS[itemId].footprint
  return targets.some((target) => {
    const targetSize = ITEMS[target.itemId].footprint
    const rowGap = Math.max(0, target.row - (row + size - 1), row - (target.row + targetSize - 1))
    const colGap = Math.max(0, target.col - (col + size - 1), col - (target.col + targetSize - 1))
    return Math.max(rowGap, colGap) <= radius
  })
}

function locationSensitiveAnnual(itemId: ItemId, row: number, col: number, parks: ReturnType<typeof listAssets>, metros: ReturnType<typeof listAssets>) {
  const item = ITEMS[itemId]
  const energy = item.energy > 0 && nearTargets(itemId, row, col, parks, 1)
    ? item.energy * ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK
    : Math.max(item.energy, 0)
  const transport = itemId === "residential"
    ? item.capacity * TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON * (nearTargets(itemId, row, col, metros, 3) ? TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO : 1)
    : 0
  return energy * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR + transport
}

/** Relocate existing assets toward useful neighbors, validating only each completed move. */
function repositionForBenefit(city: City): City {
  let current = city
  let currentTotal = computeCarbon(current, MODEL_HORIZON).total
  for (let move = 0; move < 24; move += 1) {
    const assets = listAssets(current)
    const metros = assets.filter((asset) => asset.itemId === "metro")
    const parks = assets.filter((asset) => asset.itemId === "park")
    if (!metros.length && !parks.length) break
    let best: { source: (typeof assets)[number]; destination: Position; total: number; swapWith?: (typeof assets)[number] } | null = null
    // Roads are infrastructure, not optimizer candidates. Keep their locations
    // fixed so recommendations never appear to be arbitrary new road tiles.
    const movable = assets.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId !== "road").map((asset) => {
      const residential = asset.itemId === "residential"
      const energy = ITEMS[asset.itemId].energy
      const targets = residential ? metros : parks
      const radius = residential ? 3 : 1
      const benefit = residential
        ? ITEMS.residential.capacity * TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON * (1 - TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO) * MODEL_HORIZON
        : energy * (1 - ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK) * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR * MODEL_HORIZON
      return { asset, targets, radius, benefit, eligible: (residential || energy > 0) && targets.length > 0 && !nearTargets(asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col, targets, radius) }
    }).filter((entry) => entry.eligible).sort((a, b) => b.benefit - a.benefit).slice(0, 40)

    for (const { asset, targets, radius } of movable) {
      const trial = current.map((line) => [...line])
      eraseAsset(trial, asset.row, asset.col)
      const size = ITEMS[asset.itemId].footprint
      const candidates = new Map<string, Position>()
      for (const target of targets) {
        const targetSize = ITEMS[target.itemId].footprint
        for (let row = Math.max(0, target.row - size - radius); row <= Math.min(GRID_SIZE - size, target.row + targetSize + radius); row += 1) {
          for (let col = Math.max(0, target.col - size - radius); col <= Math.min(GRID_SIZE - size, target.col + targetSize + radius); col += 1) {
            if ((row !== asset.row || col !== asset.col) && nearTargets(asset.itemId, row, col, [target], radius)) candidates.set(`${row},${col}`, { row, col })
          }
        }
      }
      const oldNearPark = nearTargets(asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col, parks, 1)
      const oldNearMetro = asset.itemId === "residential" && nearTargets(asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col, metros, 3)
      const oldAnnual = ITEMS[asset.itemId].energy * (oldNearPark ? ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK : 1) * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR
        + (asset.itemId === "residential" ? ITEMS.residential.capacity * TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON * (oldNearMetro ? TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO : 1) : 0)
      for (const destination of candidates.values()) {
        if (!canPlace(trial, asset.itemId, destination.row, destination.col)) continue
        const newNearPark = nearTargets(asset.itemId, destination.row, destination.col, parks, 1)
        const newNearMetro = asset.itemId === "residential" && nearTargets(asset.itemId, destination.row, destination.col, metros, 3)
        const newAnnual = ITEMS[asset.itemId].energy * (newNearPark ? ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK : 1) * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR
          + (asset.itemId === "residential" ? ITEMS.residential.capacity * TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON * (newNearMetro ? TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO : 1) : 0)
        const total = currentTotal - (oldAnnual - newAnnual) * MODEL_HORIZON
        if (total < currentTotal - 0.001 && (!best || total < best.total)) best = { source: asset, destination, total }
      }

      // If the useful tiles are occupied, swap with an equally sized non-road building.
      // The model checks both buildings' new park and metro effects before accepting it.
      const sourceSize = ITEMS[asset.itemId].footprint
      const oldSourceAnnual = locationSensitiveAnnual(asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col, parks, metros)
      for (const other of assets) {
        if (other.itemId === asset.itemId || other.itemId === "road" || other.itemId === "park" || other.itemId === "metro") continue
        if (ITEMS[other.itemId].footprint !== sourceSize || !nearTargets(other.itemId, other.row, other.col, targets, radius)) continue
        const oldOtherAnnual = locationSensitiveAnnual(other.itemId, other.row, other.col, parks, metros)
        const newSourceAnnual = locationSensitiveAnnual(asset.itemId, other.row, other.col, parks, metros)
        const newOtherAnnual = locationSensitiveAnnual(other.itemId, asset.row, asset.col, parks, metros)
        const total = currentTotal - (oldSourceAnnual + oldOtherAnnual - newSourceAnnual - newOtherAnnual) * MODEL_HORIZON
        if (total < currentTotal - 0.001 && (!best || total < best.total)) {
          best = { source: asset, destination: { row: other.row, col: other.col }, swapWith: other, total }
        }
      }
    }
    if (!best) break
    const next = current.map((line) => [...line])
    eraseAsset(next, best.source.row, best.source.col)
    if (best.swapWith) eraseAsset(next, best.swapWith.row, best.swapWith.col)
    if (!placeAsset(next, best.source.itemId, best.destination.row, best.destination.col)) break
    if (best.swapWith && !placeAsset(next, best.swapWith.itemId, best.source.row, best.source.col)) break
    current = next
    currentTotal = best.total
  }
  return current
}

/** Give existing solar and forest assets sensible neighborhoods when that leaves carbon unchanged. */
function repositionContextualAssets(city: City): City {
  let current = city
  let currentTotal = computeCarbon(current, MODEL_HORIZON).total
  for (let move = 0; move < 24; move += 1) {
    const assets = listAssets(current)
    let moved = false
    for (const asset of assets) {
      if (asset.itemId !== "solar" && asset.itemId !== "urbanforest") continue
      const targets = asset.itemId === "solar"
        ? assets.filter(({ itemId }) => ["industry", "datacenter", "mall", "wastewater", "busdepot"].includes(itemId))
        : assets.filter(({ itemId }) => itemId === "residential" || itemId === "office")
      // Keep renewable assets close to their purpose-built neighbors rather than merely
      // somewhere in the same district. This makes the relocation visible in dense cities.
      if (!targets.length || nearTargets(asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col, targets, 2)) continue
      const trial = current.map((line) => [...line])
      eraseAsset(trial, asset.row, asset.col)
      let destination: Position | undefined
      for (let row = 0; row <= GRID_SIZE - ITEMS[asset.itemId].footprint && !destination; row += 1) {
        for (let col = 0; col <= GRID_SIZE - ITEMS[asset.itemId].footprint; col += 1) {
          if ((row === asset.row && col === asset.col) || !nearTargets(asset.itemId, row, col, targets, 2)) continue
          if (canPlace(trial, asset.itemId, row, col)) {
            destination = { row, col }
            break
          }
        }
      }
      if (!destination || !placeAsset(trial, asset.itemId, destination.row, destination.col)) continue
      const total = computeCarbon(trial, MODEL_HORIZON).total
      if (total > currentTotal + 0.001) continue
      current = trial
      currentTotal = total
      moved = true
      break
    }
    if (!moved) break
  }
  return current
}

function candidatePositions(city: City, itemId: (typeof OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS)[number]): Position[] {
  const assets = listAssets(city)
  const ranked: Array<{ position: Position; context: number }> = []
  const targets = assets.filter((asset) => {
    if (itemId === "metro") return asset.itemId === "residential"
    if (itemId === "park") return ITEMS[asset.itemId].energy > 0 && !nearTargets(asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col, assets.filter(({ itemId: targetId }) => targetId === "park"), 1)
    if (itemId === "urbanforest") return asset.itemId === "residential" || asset.itemId === "office"
    if (itemId === "solar") return ["industry", "datacenter", "mall", "wastewater", "busdepot"].includes(asset.itemId)
    return asset.itemId !== "wind" && asset.itemId !== "solar"
  })
  const targetAreas = targets.map((asset) => ({
    row: asset.row,
    col: asset.col,
    size: ITEMS[asset.itemId].footprint,
    weight: itemId === "solar"
      ? Math.max(1, ITEMS[asset.itemId].energy / 500)
      : itemId === "park" ? Math.max(1, ITEMS[asset.itemId].energy / 500)
      : itemId === "metro" ? ITEMS.residential.capacity : 1,
  }))
  const generators = itemId === "wind" || itemId === "solar"
    ? assets.filter((asset) => asset.itemId === "wind" || asset.itemId === "solar")
    : []

  for (let row = 0; row < GRID_SIZE; row += 1) {
    for (let col = 0; col < GRID_SIZE; col += 1) {
      if (!canPlace(city, itemId, row, col)) continue
      const radius = itemId === "metro" ? 3 : itemId === "solar" || itemId === "urbanforest" ? 6 : 1
      const candidateSize = ITEMS[itemId].footprint
      const weighted = targetAreas.reduce((sum, target) => {
        const rowDistance = Math.max(0, target.row - (row + candidateSize - 1), row - (target.row + target.size - 1))
        const colDistance = Math.max(0, target.col - (col + candidateSize - 1), col - (target.col + target.size - 1))
        const distance = Math.max(rowDistance, colDistance)
        if (distance > radius) return sum
        return sum + target.weight * (radius + 1 - distance)
      }, 0)
      const generationSpacing = itemId === "wind" || itemId === "solar"
        ? generators.reduce((sum, asset) => sum + 1 / (1 + Math.max(Math.abs(asset.row - row), Math.abs(asset.col - col))), 0)
        : 0
      ranked.push({ position: { row, col }, context: weighted - generationSpacing * (itemId === "solar" ? 16 : 3) })
    }
  }

  // A few well-ranked locations are enough: carbon gains are calculated by the model,
  // while this ranking keeps solar, trees, and transit close to relevant neighbors.
  ranked.sort((a, b) => b.context - a.context)
  const count = itemId === "wind" ? 8 : 12
  return ranked.slice(0, count).map(({ position }) => position)
}

function reductionFromAddition(city: City, itemId: ItemId, row: number, col: number, assets = listAssets(city)) {
  const item = ITEMS[itemId]
  const cells = footprintCells(itemId, row, col)
  let reduction = -item.embodied
    - Math.max(item.energy, 0) * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR * MODEL_HORIZON
    + Math.max(-item.energy, 0) * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR * MODEL_HORIZON
    - item.directEmissions * MODEL_HORIZON
    + item.sink * MODEL_HORIZON

  if (itemId === "park") {
    const parks = assets.filter((asset) => asset.itemId === "park")
    assets.forEach((asset) => {
      const existing = ITEMS[asset.itemId]
      if (asset.itemId === "park" || existing.energy <= 0) return
      const targetCells = footprintCells(asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col)
      const alreadyShaded = parks.some((park) => footprintCells(park.itemId, park.row, park.col).some((parkCell) =>
        targetCells.some((target) => Math.max(Math.abs(parkCell.row - target.row), Math.abs(parkCell.col - target.col)) <= 1),
      ))
      if (alreadyShaded) return
      const shadesAsset = cells.some((cell) => targetCells.some((target) =>
        Math.max(Math.abs(cell.row - target.row), Math.abs(cell.col - target.col)) <= 1,
      ))
      if (shadesAsset) reduction += existing.energy * (1 - ENERGY_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_PARK) * GRID_EMISSIONS_FACTOR * MODEL_HORIZON
    })
  }

  if (itemId === "metro") {
    const metros = assets.filter((asset) => asset.itemId === "metro")
    assets.forEach((asset) => {
      if (asset.itemId !== "residential") return
      const targetCells = footprintCells(asset.itemId, asset.row, asset.col)
      const alreadyServed = metros.some((metro) => footprintCells(metro.itemId, metro.row, metro.col).some((metroCell) =>
        targetCells.some((target) => Math.max(Math.abs(metroCell.row - target.row), Math.abs(metroCell.col - target.col)) <= 3),
      ))
      if (alreadyServed) return
      const servesAsset = cells.some((cell) => targetCells.some((target) =>
        Math.max(Math.abs(cell.row - target.row), Math.abs(cell.col - target.col)) <= 3,
      ))
      if (servesAsset) reduction += ITEMS.residential.capacity * TRANSPORT_T_PER_PERSON * (1 - TRANSPORT_MULTIPLIER_NEAR_METRO) * MODEL_HORIZON
    })
  }

  return reduction
}

/** Adds a mixed, context-aware set of affordable assets without deleting existing buildings. */
export function optimizeCity(source: City, budget: number): City {
  const city = source.map((row) => [...row])
  const availableBudget = Number.isFinite(budget) ? Math.max(0, budget) : 0
  let spent = 0
  const added: Record<(typeof OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS)[number], number> = { solar: 0, wind: 0, urbanforest: 0, park: 0, metro: 0 }
  let currentTotal = computeCarbon(city, MODEL_HORIZON).total
  const cheapestAsset = Math.min(...OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS.map((itemId) => ITEMS[itemId].cost))
  const totalAssetLimit = OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS.reduce((sum, itemId) => sum + MAX_NEW_ASSETS[itemId], 0)
  const maxAdditions = Math.min(city.flat().filter((cell) => cell === null).length, totalAssetLimit, Math.floor(availableBudget / cheapestAsset))

  for (let addition = 0; addition < maxAdditions; addition += 1) {
    let best: { itemId: (typeof OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS)[number]; row: number; col: number; benefitPerCost: number; total: number } | null = null
    const assets = listAssets(city)

    for (const itemId of OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS) {
      if (added[itemId] >= MAX_NEW_ASSETS[itemId]) continue
      const cost = ITEMS[itemId].cost
      if (spent + cost > availableBudget) continue

      for (const { row, col } of candidatePositions(city, itemId)) {
        if (!canPlace(city, itemId, row, col)) continue
        const benefit = reductionFromAddition(city, itemId, row, col, assets)
        const candidateTotal = currentTotal - benefit
        const benefitPerCost = benefit / cost
        if (benefitPerCost > 0 && (!best || benefitPerCost > best.benefitPerCost)) {
          best = { itemId, row, col, benefitPerCost, total: candidateTotal }
        }
      }
    }

    // Stop when no affordable placement reduces the modeled 30-year carbon total.
    if (!best) break
    placeAsset(city, best.itemId, best.row, best.col)
    added[best.itemId] += 1
    spent += ITEMS[best.itemId].cost
    currentTotal = best.total
  }

  return repositionContextualAssets(repositionForBenefit(city))
}

/** Improves the 30-year estimate toward the requested reduction, then relocates assets where useful. */
export function optimizeCityForReduction(source: City, targetPercent: number): City {
  const city = source.map((row) => [...row])
  const requested = Number.isFinite(targetPercent) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(99, targetPercent)) : 0
  const baseline = computeCarbon(city, MODEL_HORIZON).total
  if (baseline <= 0 || requested === 0) return repositionContextualAssets(city)
  const targetTotal = baseline * (1 - requested / 100)
  let currentTotal = baseline
  const added: Record<(typeof OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS)[number], number> = { solar: 0, wind: 0, urbanforest: 0, park: 0, metro: 0 }

  for (let addition = 0; addition < GRID_SIZE * GRID_SIZE && currentTotal > targetTotal; addition += 1) {
    let best: { itemId: (typeof OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS)[number]; row: number; col: number; total: number; benefitPerCost: number } | null = null
    const assets = listAssets(city)

    for (const itemId of OPTIMIZABLE_ASSETS) {
      if (added[itemId] >= MAX_NEW_ASSETS[itemId]) continue
      for (const { row, col } of candidatePositions(city, itemId)) {
        if (!canPlace(city, itemId, row, col)) continue
        const reduction = reductionFromAddition(city, itemId, row, col, assets)
        const total = currentTotal - reduction
        const benefitPerCost = reduction / Math.max(ITEMS[itemId].cost, 0.01)
        if (benefitPerCost > 0 && (!best || benefitPerCost > best.benefitPerCost)) {
          best = { itemId, row, col, total, benefitPerCost }
        }
      }
    }

    if (!best) break
    placeAsset(city, best.itemId, best.row, best.col)
    added[best.itemId] += 1
    currentTotal = best.total
  }

  return repositionContextualAssets(repositionForBenefit(city))
}


================================================================
FILE: src\lib\optimizer.worker.ts
================================================================

import { optimizeCity, optimizeCityForReduction } from "./optimizer.ts"
import type { City } from "./carbon.ts"

type Request = { city: City; mode: "budget"; value: number } | { city: City; mode: "reduction" | "minimum"; value: number }

self.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<Request>) => {
  const { city, mode, value } = event.data
  const optimized = mode === "budget" ? optimizeCity(city, value) : optimizeCityForReduction(city, mode === "minimum" ? 99 : value)
  self.postMessage(optimized)
}


================================================================
FILE: src\store\useCityStore.ts
================================================================

import { create } from "zustand"
import { ITEM_IDS, ITEMS, type ItemId } from "../data/items.ts"
import { computeCarbon, MODEL_HORIZON, type City } from "../lib/carbon.ts"
import { eraseAsset, GRID_SIZE, placeAsset } from "../lib/grid.ts"
import { optimizeCity, optimizeCityForReduction } from "../lib/optimizer.ts"

type Tool = "place" | "erase"

type CityState = {
  city: City
  selectedItem: ItemId
  tool: Tool
  year: number
  savedLayoutA: City | null
  savedLayoutB: City | null
  reductionResult: { targetPercent: number; achievedPercent: number; targetMet: boolean; mode: "target" | "minimum" } | null
  optimizationStatus: "idle" | "running"
  setSelectedItem: (item: ItemId) => void
  setTool: (tool: Tool) => void
  setYear: (year: number) => void
  placeAt: (row: number, col: number) => void
  eraseAt: (row: number, col: number) => void
  resetCity: () => void
  loadStarter: () => void
  saveLayout: (slot: "A" | "B") => void
  optimize: (budget: number) => void
  optimizeForReduction: (targetPercent: number) => void
  optimizeInWorker: (mode: "budget" | "reduction" | "minimum", value: number) => Promise<void>
}

const emptyCity = (): City =>
  Array.from({ length: GRID_SIZE }, () => Array<ItemId | null>(GRID_SIZE).fill(null))
const cloneCity = (city: City): City => city.map((row) => [...row])

function starterCity() {
  const city = emptyCity()
  // A fresh seed gives each starter load a different street/block arrangement.
  // The asset counts and footprint validity remain fixed for fair comparisons.
  let seed = (Date.now() ^ Math.floor(Math.random() * 0xffffffff)) >>> 0
  const random = () => {
    seed = (seed * 1664525 + 1013904223) >>> 0
    return seed / 0x100000000
  }
  for (const road of [9, 19, 29]) {
    for (let index = 0; index < GRID_SIZE; index += 1) {
      city[road][index] = "road"
      city[index][road] = "road"
    }
  }

  const placeAtVariedPosition = (itemId: ItemId) => {
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 32; attempt += 1) {
      const row = Math.floor(random() * GRID_SIZE)
      const col = Math.floor(random() * GRID_SIZE)
      if (placeAsset(city, itemId, row, col)) return true
    }
    for (let row = 0; row < GRID_SIZE; row += 1) {
      for (let col = 0; col < GRID_SIZE; col += 1) {
        if (placeAsset(city, itemId, row, col)) return true
      }
    }
    return false
  }

  // Keep every asset type represented while reducing the previous 547-tile
  // starter by one quarter to 410 tiles; roads remain on the same main avenues.
  const groups: Array<{ count: number; items: ItemId[] }> = [
    { count: 28, items: ["industry", "hospital", "datacenter", "mall", "landfill", "busdepot"] },
    { count: 57, items: ["water", "school", "wastewater", "hotel", "urbanforest", "wind", "solar", "park"] },
    { count: 94, items: ["residential", "office", "residential", "residential", "metro"] },
  ]
  const planned: ItemId[] = []
  for (const group of groups) {
    for (let index = 0; index < group.count; index += 1) {
      const itemId = group.items[(index * (group.items.length === 5 ? 2 : 5)) % group.items.length]
      planned.push(itemId)
    }
  }
  planned.sort((a, b) => ITEMS[b].footprint - ITEMS[a].footprint || random() - 0.5)
  for (const itemId of planned) {
    if (!placeAtVariedPosition(itemId)) throw new Error(`Trial city could not place ${itemId}`)
  }
  return city
}

export const useCityStore = create<CityState>((set, get) => ({
  city: emptyCity(),
  selectedItem: "residential",
  tool: "place",
  year: MODEL_HORIZON,
  savedLayoutA: null,
  savedLayoutB: null,
  reductionResult: null,
  optimizationStatus: "idle",
  setSelectedItem: (selectedItem) => set({ selectedItem, tool: "place" }),
  setTool: (tool) => set({ tool }),
  setYear: (year) => set({ year: Math.max(0, Math.min(MODEL_HORIZON, year)) }),
  placeAt: (row, col) =>
    set((state) => {
      const city = cloneCity(state.city)
      if (row < 0 || col < 0 || row >= city.length || col >= city[row].length) return state
      if (state.tool === "erase") {
        if (!eraseAsset(city, row, col)) return state
      } else if (!placeAsset(city, state.selectedItem, row, col)) return state
      return { city, savedLayoutA: cloneCity(city), reductionResult: null }
    }),
  eraseAt: (row, col) => set((state) => {
    const city = cloneCity(state.city)
    if (!eraseAsset(city, row, col)) return state
    return { city, savedLayoutA: cloneCity(city), reductionResult: null }
  }),
  resetCity: () =>
    set({ city: emptyCity(), savedLayoutA: null, savedLayoutB: null, reductionResult: null, optimizationStatus: "idle" }),
  loadStarter: () => set(() => {
    const city = starterCity()
    return { city, savedLayoutA: cloneCity(city), savedLayoutB: null, reductionResult: null, optimizationStatus: "idle" }
  }),
  saveLayout: (slot) =>
    set((state) =>
      slot === "A"
        ? { savedLayoutA: cloneCity(state.city) }
        : { savedLayoutB: cloneCity(state.city) },
    ),
  optimize: (budget) => {
    const before = cloneCity(get().city)
    const optimized = optimizeCity(before, budget)
    set({ city: cloneCity(optimized), savedLayoutA: before, savedLayoutB: cloneCity(optimized), reductionResult: null })
  },
  optimizeForReduction: (targetPercent) => {
    const before = cloneCity(get().city)
    const baseline = computeCarbon(before, MODEL_HORIZON).total
    const optimized = optimizeCityForReduction(before, targetPercent)
    const after = computeCarbon(optimized, MODEL_HORIZON).total
    const achievedPercent = baseline > 0 ? ((baseline - after) / baseline) * 100 : 0
    const target = Math.max(0, Math.min(99, Number.isFinite(targetPercent) ? targetPercent : 0))
    set({
      city: cloneCity(optimized),
      savedLayoutA: before,
      savedLayoutB: cloneCity(optimized),
      reductionResult: { targetPercent: target, achievedPercent, targetMet: achievedPercent >= target, mode: "target" },
    })
  },
  optimizeInWorker: async (mode, value) => {
    const sourceCity = get().city
    const before = cloneCity(sourceCity)
    set({ optimizationStatus: "running" })
    let optimized: City
    const reductionTarget = mode === "minimum" ? 99 : value
    const optimizeOnMainThread = () => mode === "budget" ? optimizeCity(before, value) : optimizeCityForReduction(before, reductionTarget)
    try {
      if (typeof Worker === "undefined") optimized = optimizeOnMainThread()
      else optimized = await new Promise<City>((resolve) => {
        const worker = new Worker(new URL("../lib/optimizer.worker.ts", import.meta.url), { type: "module" })
        const finish = (city: City) => { worker.terminate(); resolve(city) }
        worker.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<City>) => finish(event.data)
        worker.onerror = () => finish(optimizeOnMainThread())
        worker.postMessage({ city: before, mode, value })
      })
    } catch {
      optimized = optimizeOnMainThread()
    }
    if (get().city !== sourceCity) {
      set({ optimizationStatus: "idle" })
      return
    }
    if (mode === "budget") {
      set({ city: cloneCity(optimized), savedLayoutA: before, savedLayoutB: cloneCity(optimized), reductionResult: null, optimizationStatus: "idle" })
      return
    }
    const baseline = computeCarbon(before, MODEL_HORIZON).total
    const after = computeCarbon(optimized, MODEL_HORIZON).total
    const achievedPercent = baseline > 0 ? ((baseline - after) / baseline) * 100 : 0
    const target = mode === "minimum" ? 99 : Math.max(0, Math.min(99, Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0))
    set({ city: cloneCity(optimized), savedLayoutA: before, savedLayoutB: cloneCity(optimized), reductionResult: { targetPercent: target, achievedPercent, targetMet: mode === "minimum" || achievedPercent >= target, mode: mode === "minimum" ? "minimum" : "target" }, optimizationStatus: "idle" })
  },
}))

export { ITEM_IDS, computeCarbon }
